<script>/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: CORE ENGINE
   Vector math, physics bodies, collision, core game state
   ============================================================ */

const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }
function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
function rand(a, b) { return a + Math.random() * (b - a); }
function randInt(a, b) { return Math.floor(rand(a, b + 1)); }
function choice(arr) { return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)]; }
function dist(x1, y1, x2, y2) { return Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1); }
function angleTo(x1, y1, x2, y2) { return Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1); }

class Vec2 {
  constructor(x = 0, y = 0) { this.x = x; this.y = y; }
  set(x, y) { this.x = x; this.y = y; return this; }
  copy() { return new Vec2(this.x, this.y); }
  add(v) { this.x += v.x; this.y += v.y; return this; }
  sub(v) { this.x -= v.x; this.y -= v.y; return this; }
  scale(s) { this.x *= s; this.y *= s; return this; }
  len() { return Math.hypot(this.x, this.y); }
  norm() { const l = this.len() || 1; this.x /= l; this.y /= l; return this; }
  dot(v) { return this.x * v.x + this.y * v.y; }
}

/* ---------------- Ball ---------------- */
class Ball {
  constructor(game, opts = {}) {
    this.game = game;
    this.radius = opts.radius || 9;
    this.baseRadius = this.radius;
    this.reset(opts.dir || (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1));
    this.trail = [];
    this.maxTrail = 14;
    this.color = opts.color || '#00f0ff';
    this.spinFactor = 0;
    this.id = opts.id || 'ball_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
    this.ghost = !!opts.ghost;
    this.alive = true;
    this.gravityWells = [];
    this.curve = 0; // active curve power-up
    this.frozenTimer = 0; // Ice Lock ability
    this.wobble = 0;
    this.colorFlickerUntil = 0;
    this.thunderChargeUntil = 0; // Thunder's persistent electrical aura
    this.dragonTrail = false;
    // Chronos rewind: a longer, coarser position/velocity history than the
    // short visual trail, sampled a few times a second so a rewind can
    // genuinely retrace the ball's real path rather than jump to one point.
    this.posHistory = [];
    this.posHistoryTimer = 0;
    this.rewindPlayback = null; // {frames, index} while a Chronos rewind is animating
    this.roleAuraColor = null;
    this.roleAuraUntil = 0;
  }
  setRoleAura(color, durationMs) {
    this.roleAuraColor = color;
    this.roleAuraUntil = performance.now() + durationMs;
  }
  reset(dir = 1) {
    const g = this.game;
    this.x = g.width / 2;
    this.y = g.height / 2;
    const angle = rand(-0.28, 0.28) * Math.PI + (dir < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
    const speed = g.settings.ballSpeed;
    this.vx = Math.cos(angle) * speed;
    this.vy = Math.sin(angle) * speed;
    this.speed = speed;
    this.radius = this.baseRadius;
    this.trail = [];
    this.curve = 0;
    this.spinFactor = 0;
  }
  currentSpeed() { return Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy); }
  normalizeSpeed(target) {
    const s = this.currentSpeed() || 1;
    const k = target / s;
    this.vx *= k; this.vy *= k;
  }
  update(dt) {
    // Chronos rewind: replay recorded history backward, overriding normal
    // physics until the animated playback reaches its target frame.
    if (this.rewindPlayback) {
      const rp = this.rewindPlayback;
      rp.elapsed += dt;
      const t = clamp(rp.elapsed / rp.duration, 0, 1);
      const idx = Math.floor(t * (rp.frames.length - 1));
      const frame = rp.frames[idx];
      this.x = frame.x; this.y = frame.y;
      this.trail.unshift({ x: this.x, y: this.y, r: this.radius });
      if (this.trail.length > this.maxTrail) this.trail.pop();
      if (t >= 1) {
        this.vx = rp.frames[rp.frames.length - 1].vx;
        this.vy = rp.frames[rp.frames.length - 1].vy;
        this.rewindPlayback = null;
        if (this.game && this.game.fx) this.game.fx.ring(this.x, this.y, '#ffd23f');
      }
      return;
    }
    // Ice Lock freeze: the ball holds completely still until it thaws
    if (this.frozenTimer > 0) {
      this.frozenTimer -= dt;
      this.trail.unshift({ x: this.x, y: this.y, r: this.radius });
      if (this.trail.length > this.maxTrail) this.trail.pop();
      if (this.frozenTimer <= 0) {
        this.frozenTimer = 0;
        if (this.game && this.game.fx) this.game.fx.ring(this.x, this.y, '#8fdcff');
      }
      return;
    }
    // trail
    this.trail.unshift({ x: this.x, y: this.y, r: this.radius });
    if (this.trail.length > this.maxTrail) this.trail.pop();

    // longer-depth history for Chronos rewind, sampled ~12x/sec
    this.posHistoryTimer -= dt;
    if (this.posHistoryTimer <= 0) {
      this.posHistoryTimer = 1 / 12;
      this.posHistory.push({ x: this.x, y: this.y, vx: this.vx, vy: this.vy });
      if (this.posHistory.length > 36) this.posHistory.shift(); // ~3 seconds of history
    }

    // curve ball effect (magnus-esque)
    if (this.curve !== 0) {
      const perp = { x: -this.vy, y: this.vx };
      const pl = Math.hypot(perp.x, perp.y) || 1;
      this.vx += (perp.x / pl) * this.curve * dt;
      this.vy += (perp.y / pl) * this.curve * dt;
    }

    // gravity wells (power-up)
    for (const w of this.gravityWells) {
      const dx = w.x - this.x, dy = w.y - this.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      if (d < w.radius) {
        const f = w.strength * (1 - d / w.radius) * dt;
        this.vx += (dx / d) * f;
        this.vy += (dy / d) * f;
      }
    }

    this.x += this.vx * dt;
    this.y += this.vy * dt;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    const g = this.game;
    const now = performance.now();
    const hasAura = this.roleAuraUntil && now < this.roleAuraUntil;
    const auraColor = hasAura ? this.roleAuraColor : null;

    // trail — recolored toward the role's aura color when one is active,
    // but the ball's own glow (drawn last, below) always stays on top and
    // fully opaque so it never gets lost in the effect.
    for (let i = this.trail.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const t = this.trail[i];
      const a = (1 - i / this.trail.length) * (hasAura ? 0.55 : 0.35);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(auraColor || this.color, a);
      ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, t.r * (1 - i / this.trail.length * 0.4) * (hasAura ? 1.15 : 1), 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    if (hasAura) {
      // extra outer aura ring so role-driven trails read as a distinct
      // effect layer rather than just a recolored default trail
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35 + Math.sin(now / 90) * 0.1;
      ctx.strokeStyle = auraColor;
      ctx.shadowColor = auraColor;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, this.radius + 7, 0, TAU);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // glow — always drawn last, fully opaque, so the ball is never obscured
    ctx.save();
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 22 * g.settings.glowIntensity;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.fillStyle = this.ghost ? hexToRgba(this.color, 0.4) : this.color;
    ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, this.radius, 0, TAU);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

function hexToRgba(hex, a) {
  hex = hex.replace('#', '');
  if (hex.length === 3) hex = hex.split('').map(c => c + c).join('');
  const r = parseInt(hex.substr(0, 2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(hex.substr(2, 2), 16);
  const b = parseInt(hex.substr(4, 2), 16);
  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a})`;
}

/* ---------------- Paddle ---------------- */
class Paddle {
  constructor(game, side, opts = {}) {
    this.game = game;
    this.side = side; // 'left' | 'right' | 'top' | 'bottom'
    this.width = opts.width || 14;
    this.height = opts.height || 100;
    this.color = opts.color || (side === 'left' || side === 'top' ? '#ff00d4' : '#00f0ff');
    this.speed = game.settings.paddleSpeed;
    this.score = 0;
    this.lives = game.settings.lives || 0;
    this.vy = 0;
    this.trail = [];
    this.shieldHits = 0;
    this.shrink = 1;
    this.stunTimer = 0;
    this.magnetTimer = 0;
    this.giantTimer = 0;
    this.dashTimer = 0;
    this.dashMul = 1;
    this.wallTimer = 0;
    this.titanTimer = 0;
    this.twinTimer = 0;
    this.sniperCharge = false;
    this.nextHitPowerMul = 1;
    this.nextAngleMul = 1;
    this.nextSpeedMul = 1;
    this.nextSlowReturn = false;
    this.sizeBoostTimer = 0;
    this.braceTimer = 0;
    this.echoTimer = 0;
    this.echoY = 0;
    this.focusTimer = 0;
    this.guardCharge = 0;
    this.sparkNextHit = false;
    this.x2Timer = 0;
    this.flareUntilPrimary = 0;
    this.flareUntilBonus = 0;
    this.abilityAnim = null;
    this.vortexWell = null;
    this.roleController = null; // assigned by game setup once RoleController is available
    this.reset();
  }
  reset() {
    const g = this.game;
    const margin = 28;
    if (this.side === 'left') { this.x = margin; this.y = g.height / 2 - this.height / 2; }
    else if (this.side === 'right') { this.x = g.width - margin - this.width; this.y = g.height / 2 - this.height / 2; }
  }
  centerY() { return this.y + this.height / 2; }
  effectiveHeight() {
    const roleSizeMul = (this.roleController && this.roleController.role.passive.sizeMul) || 1;
    const rookieBoost = this.sizeBoostTimer > 0 ? 1.25 : 1;
    return this.height * this.shrink * (this.giantTimer > 0 ? 1.6 : 1) * roleSizeMul * rookieBoost;
  }
  moveTo(targetCenterY, dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    const h = this.effectiveHeight();
    let target = clamp(targetCenterY - h / 2, 0, g.height - h);
    this.y = target;
  }
  moveBy(dy, dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    const h = this.effectiveHeight();
    if (this.stunTimer > 0) return;
    const roleSpeedMul = (this.roleController && this.roleController.role.passive.speedMul) || 1;
    const effSpeed = this.speed * (this.dashMul || 1) * roleSpeedMul;
    this.y = clamp(this.y + dy * effSpeed * dt, 0, g.height - h);
  }
  update(dt) {
    if (this.stunTimer > 0) this.stunTimer -= dt;
    if (this.magnetTimer > 0) this.magnetTimer -= dt;
    if (this.giantTimer > 0) this.giantTimer -= dt;
    if (this.shrink < 1) this.shrink = Math.min(1, this.shrink + dt * 0.3);
  }
  rect() {
    return { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: this.width, h: this.effectiveHeight() };
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    const g = this.game;
    const r = this.rect();
    ctx.save();
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 18 * g.settings.glowIntensity * (this.stunTimer > 0 ? 0.3 : 1);
    ctx.fillStyle = this.stunTimer > 0 ? hexToRgba(this.color, 0.35) : this.color;
    roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5);
    ctx.fill();
    if (this.giantTimer > 0) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba('#ffffff', 0.6);
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x - 3, r.y - 3, r.w + 6, r.h + 6, 7);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

function roundRect(ctx, x, y, w, h, r) {
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
  ctx.closePath();
}

/* ---------------- AABB / circle collision helpers ---------------- */
function circleRectCollide(cx, cy, cr, rx, ry, rw, rh) {
  const nx = clamp(cx, rx, rx + rw);
  const ny = clamp(cy, ry, ry + rh);
  const dx = cx - nx, dy = cy - ny;
  return (dx * dx + dy * dy) <= cr * cr;
}

function resolveBallPaddle(ball, paddle) {
  const r = paddle.rect();
  const nx = clamp(ball.x, r.x, r.x + r.w);
  const ny = clamp(ball.y, r.y, r.y + r.h);
  const dx = ball.x - nx, dy = ball.y - ny;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.01;
  if (d > ball.radius) return false;
  // push out
  const overlap = ball.radius - d;
  ball.x += (dx / d) * overlap;
  ball.y += (dy / d) * overlap;

  const dirSign = paddle.side === 'left' ? 1 : -1;
  const rolePassive = (paddle.roleController && paddle.roleController.role.passive) || {};
  const hitPowerMul = (rolePassive.hitPowerMul || 1) * (paddle.nextHitPowerMul || 1);
  const angleMul = (rolePassive.angleMul || 1) * (paddle.nextAngleMul || 1);
  const oneShotSpeedMul = paddle.nextSpeedMul || 1;
  const slowReturn = !!paddle.nextSlowReturn;
  const braceReduction = paddle.braceTimer > 0 ? 0.55 : 1; // Defender's Brace: softens the incoming hit's power
  const guardReduction = paddle.guardCharge > 0 ? 0.3 : 1; // Guard Up: heavily weakens the next incoming hit, one-time use
  if (paddle.guardCharge > 0) paddle.guardCharge = 0;
  // one-shot "next hit" modifiers consume themselves once used
  paddle.nextHitPowerMul = 1; paddle.nextAngleMul = 1; paddle.nextSpeedMul = 1; paddle.nextSlowReturn = false;

  let speed = ball.currentSpeed() * braceReduction * guardReduction;
  speed = Math.min(speed * 1.045 * hitPowerMul * oneShotSpeedMul * (slowReturn ? 0.75 : 1), ball.game.settings.maxBallSpeed);
  speed = Math.max(speed, 90); // never let a slow-modifier stall the ball completely

  if (paddle.sniperCharge) {
    // Sniper-style ability: locked flat, hard-to-read straight shot
    ball.vx = dirSign * speed * 1.3;
    ball.vy = 0;
    paddle.sniperCharge = false;
    if (ball.game && ball.game.fx) ball.game.fx.text(ball.x, ball.y - 20, 'SNIPED!', '#3aa9ff', 16);
  } else {
    // reflect based on hit position (adds angle control)
    let relative = ((ball.y - (r.y + r.h / 2)) / (r.h / 2)) * angleMul;
    relative = clamp(relative, -1, 1);
    const bounceAngle = relative * (Math.PI / 3.2); // max ~56deg
    ball.vx = Math.cos(bounceAngle) * speed * dirSign;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(bounceAngle) * speed;
    // add paddle vy influence (spin)
    ball.vy += paddle.vy * 0.18;
  }

  if (paddle.sparkNextHit) {
    paddle.sparkNextHit = false;
    if (ball.game && ball.game.fx) ball.game.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, '#9fb4c7', 10, { maxSpeed: 160 });
  }

  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: FX SYSTEM
   Particles, screen shake, floating text, procedural WebAudio SFX
   ============================================================ */

class Particle {
  constructor(x, y, vx, vy, color, life, size) {
    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.color = color; this.life = life; this.maxLife = life;
    this.size = size; this.gravity = 0;
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.x += this.vx * dt;
    this.y += this.vy * dt;
    this.vy += this.gravity * dt;
    this.vx *= 0.98;
    this.vy *= 0.98;
    this.life -= dt;
    return this.life > 0;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    const a = Math.max(0, this.life / this.maxLife);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = a;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, this.size * a, 0, TAU);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

class FloatText {
  constructor(x, y, text, color, size = 22) {
    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.text = text; this.color = color;
    this.life = 1.1; this.maxLife = 1.1; this.size = size;
    this.vy = -40;
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.y += this.vy * dt;
    this.vy *= 0.96;
    this.life -= dt;
    return this.life > 0;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    const a = Math.max(0, this.life / this.maxLife);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = a;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
    ctx.font = `800 ${this.size}px 'Orbitron', sans-serif`;
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(this.text, this.x, this.y);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

class FXManager {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.particles = [];
    this.floatTexts = [];
    this.shakeMag = 0;
    this.shakeDecay = 0;
    this.flashAlpha = 0;
    this.flashColor = '#ffffff';
    this.hitStop = 0; // brief freeze frames for impact
  }
  burst(x, y, color, count = 18, opts = {}) {
    const g = this.game;
    if (g && g.settings && g.settings.reducedMotion) {
      count = Math.max(1, Math.round(count * 0.3));
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const a = rand(0, TAU);
      const speed = rand(opts.minSpeed || 60, opts.maxSpeed || 260);
      const p = new Particle(x, y, Math.cos(a) * speed, Math.sin(a) * speed, color,
        rand(opts.minLife || 0.35, opts.maxLife || 0.9), rand(2, opts.size || 5));
      p.gravity = opts.gravity || 0;
      this.particles.push(p);
    }
  }
  ring(x, y, color) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const a = (i / 26) * TAU;
      const speed = 180;
      this.particles.push(new Particle(x, y, Math.cos(a) * speed, Math.sin(a) * speed, color, 0.5, 3));
    }
  }
  text(x, y, text, color, size) {
    this.floatTexts.push(new FloatText(x, y, text, color, size));
  }
  shake(mag, decay = 4) {
    if (this.game && this.game.settings && this.game.settings.reducedMotion) mag *= 0.25;
    this.shakeMag = Math.max(this.shakeMag, mag);
    this.shakeDecay = decay;
  }
  flash(color = '#ffffff', a = 0.35) {
    this.flashAlpha = a;
    this.flashColor = color;
  }
  freeze(t) {
    this.hitStop = Math.max(this.hitStop, t);
  }
  update(dt) {
    if (this.hitStop > 0) { this.hitStop -= dt; return false; }
    this.particles = this.particles.filter(p => p.update(dt));
    this.floatTexts = this.floatTexts.filter(t => t.update(dt));
    if (this.shakeMag > 0) this.shakeMag = Math.max(0, this.shakeMag - this.shakeDecay * dt * 60 * dt);
    if (this.flashAlpha > 0) this.flashAlpha = Math.max(0, this.flashAlpha - dt * 1.6);
    return true;
  }
  getShakeOffset() {
    if (this.shakeMag <= 0) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    return { x: rand(-this.shakeMag, this.shakeMag), y: rand(-this.shakeMag, this.shakeMag) };
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    for (const p of this.particles) p.draw(ctx);
    for (const t of this.floatTexts) t.draw(ctx);
  }
  drawFlash(ctx, w, h) {
    if (this.flashAlpha <= 0) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = this.flashAlpha;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.flashColor;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/* ---------------- Procedural Audio ---------------- */
class SFX {
  constructor() {
    this.ctx = null;
    this.master = null;
    this.enabled = true;
    this.volume = 0.5;
    this.musicNodes = [];
    this.musicPlaying = false;
  }
  ensure() {
    if (this.ctx) return;
    try {
      this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      this.master = this.ctx.createGain();
      this.master.gain.value = this.volume;
      this.master.connect(this.ctx.destination);
    } catch (e) { this.enabled = false; }
  }
  resume() { if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume(); }
  setVolume(v) { this.volume = v; if (this.master) this.master.gain.value = v; }

  tone(freq, dur, type = 'sine', opts = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.ensure();
    const t0 = this.ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = type;
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t0);
    if (opts.slideTo) osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, opts.slideTo), t0 + dur);
    const peak = opts.gain !== undefined ? opts.gain : 0.3;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(peak, t0 + 0.008);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur);
    osc.connect(gain).connect(this.master);
    osc.start(t0);
    osc.stop(t0 + dur + 0.02);
  }
  noise(dur, opts = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.ensure();
    const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * dur;
    const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / bufferSize);
    const src = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buffer;
    const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    filter.type = opts.filterType || 'lowpass';
    filter.frequency.value = opts.filterFreq || 2000;
    const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.value = opts.gain !== undefined ? opts.gain : 0.25;
    src.connect(filter).connect(gain).connect(this.master);
    src.start();
  }
  paddleHit(pitch = 1) { this.tone(220 * pitch, 0.09, 'square', { gain: 0.22, slideTo: 340 * pitch }); }
  wallHit() { this.tone(140, 0.07, 'triangle', { gain: 0.18, slideTo: 90 }); }
  score(win) { 
    if (win) {
      [523, 659, 784, 1046].forEach((f, i) => setTimeout(() => this.tone(f, 0.25, 'sine', { gain: 0.28 }), i * 90));
    } else {
      this.tone(180, 0.3, 'sawtooth', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 60 });
    }
  }
  powerup() { [660, 880, 1100].forEach((f, i) => setTimeout(() => this.tone(f, 0.12, 'sine', { gain: 0.22 }), i * 55)); }
  countdown(final) { this.tone(final ? 880 : 440, 0.15, 'square', { gain: 0.25 }); }
  menuBlip() { this.tone(500, 0.05, 'square', { gain: 0.15 }); }
  menuConfirm() { this.tone(700, 0.08, 'square', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 900 }); }
  explosion() { this.noise(0.4, { filterFreq: 800, gain: 0.3 }); this.tone(80, 0.4, 'sawtooth', { gain: 0.25, slideTo: 30 }); }
  shieldBreak() { this.noise(0.25, { filterFreq: 3000, gain: 0.25 }); }
  toggleMusic(on) {
    this.musicPlaying = on;
    if (on) this.startMusic(); else this.stopMusic();
  }
  startMusic() {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.ensure();
    this.stopMusic();
    const notes = [110, 130.81, 146.83, 110, 164.81, 146.83, 130.81, 98];
    let i = 0;
    this.musicTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (!this.musicPlaying) return;
      const f = notes[i % notes.length];
      this.tone(f, 0.35, 'triangle', { gain: 0.06 });
      if (i % 2 === 0) this.tone(f * 2, 0.15, 'sine', { gain: 0.03 });
      i++;
    }, 380);
  }
  stopMusic() {
    if (this.musicTimer) clearInterval(this.musicTimer);
    this.musicTimer = null;
  }
}

   NEON PONG :: POWER-UP SYSTEM
   ============================================================ */

const POWERUP_TYPES = [
  { id: 'multiball', label: 'MULTIBALL', icon: '⚡', color: '#ffe600', desc: 'Splits ball into 3' },
  { id: 'giant', label: 'GIANT PADDLE', icon: '▲', color: '#00ff88', desc: 'Your paddle grows' },
  { id: 'shrink', label: 'SHRINK RAY', icon: '▼', color: '#ff2266', desc: 'Enemy paddle shrinks' },
  { id: 'speedup', label: 'OVERDRIVE', icon: '»', color: '#ff8800', desc: 'Ball speeds up' },
  { id: 'slowmo', label: 'SLOW-MO', icon: '«', color: '#00e5ff', desc: 'Ball slows down' },
  { id: 'curve', label: 'CURVEBALL', icon: '~', color: '#c400ff', desc: 'Ball starts curving' },
  { id: 'shield', label: 'SHIELD', icon: '◆', color: '#00fff0', desc: 'Block one goal' },
  { id: 'invert', label: 'INVERT', icon: '↕', color: '#ff00aa', desc: 'Flips enemy controls' },
  { id: 'ghost', label: 'GHOST BALL', icon: '○', color: '#ffffff', desc: 'Ball turns translucent' },
  { id: 'magnet', label: 'MAGNET', icon: '☯', color: '#ffd700', desc: 'Attracts the ball' },
  { id: 'gravity', label: 'GRAVITY WELL', icon: '◉', color: '#8000ff', desc: 'Pulls ball off course' },
  { id: 'stun', label: 'STUN', icon: '!', color: '#ff0000', desc: 'Freezes enemy briefly' },
  { id: 'tripleball', label: 'TRIPLE THREAT', icon: '☄', color: '#ff5500', desc: 'Splits ball into 4' },
  { id: 'megapaddle', label: 'MEGA PADDLE', icon: '█', color: '#39ff14', desc: 'Paddle doubles, briefly' },
  { id: 'mirror', label: 'MIRROR', icon: '⇄', color: '#00bfff', desc: 'Swaps both paddle positions' },
  { id: 'freeze', label: 'DEEP FREEZE', icon: '❄', color: '#7df9ff', desc: 'Ball slows to a crawl' },
  { id: 'blackhole', label: 'BLACK HOLE', icon: '⬤', color: '#1a0033', desc: 'Strong pull to center' },
  { id: 'bonus', label: 'BONUS POINT', icon: '★', color: '#ffd23f', desc: 'Instant free point' },
  { id: 'barrier', label: 'BARRIER', icon: '▤', color: '#66ffcc', desc: 'Temp wall at mid-court' },
  { id: 'chaos_orb', label: 'CHAOS', icon: '✦', color: '#ff00ff', desc: 'Triggers a random effect' },
];

/* Curated subset used by Orbs Mode, which leans into power-up chaos
   more heavily than normal modes and spawns orbs much faster. */
const ORBS_MODE_POOL = POWERUP_TYPES.map(t => t.id);


class PowerupOrb {
  constructor(game, type, x, y) {
    this.game = game;
    this.type = type;
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.radius = 16;
    this.age = 0;
    this.life = 12;
    this.bobPhase = rand(0, TAU);
    this.collected = false;
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.age += dt;
    return this.age < this.life;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    const bob = Math.sin(this.age * 3 + this.bobPhase) * 4;
    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(this.age * 6) * 0.08;
    const fade = this.age > this.life - 2 ? Math.max(0, (this.life - this.age) / 2) : 1;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = fade;
    ctx.translate(this.x, this.y + bob);
    ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);
    ctx.shadowColor = this.type.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
    ctx.strokeStyle = this.type.color;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 0, this.radius, 0, TAU);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(this.type.color, 0.15);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = this.type.color;
    ctx.font = '700 16px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText(this.type.icon, 0, 1);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

class PowerupManager {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.orbs = [];
    this.spawnTimer = rand(4, 7);
    this.activeEffects = []; // {type, target, timer}
  }
  reset() {
    this.orbs = [];
    this.activeEffects = [];
    this.spawnTimer = rand(4, 7);
  }
  update(dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    if (!g.settings.powerupsEnabled && g.mode !== 'orbs') { this.orbs = []; return; }
    const isOrbsMode = g.mode === 'orbs';
    const maxOrbs = isOrbsMode ? 6 : 3;
    this.spawnTimer -= dt;
    if (this.spawnTimer <= 0 && this.orbs.length < maxOrbs) {
      this.spawnOrb();
      this.spawnTimer = isOrbsMode ? rand(1.5, 3) : rand(5, 9);
    }
    this.orbs = this.orbs.filter(o => o.update(dt));

    // check collision with any ball (snapshot balls so newly-spawned balls from
    // effects like multiball don't get visited in the same pass and re-trigger)
    const ballsSnapshot = g.balls.slice();
    for (const orb of this.orbs) {
      if (orb.age >= orb.life) continue; // already collected this frame
      for (const ball of ballsSnapshot) {
        if (orb.age >= orb.life) break; // stop once this orb has been collected
        if (dist(ball.x, ball.y, orb.x, orb.y) < orb.radius + ball.radius) {
          this.collect(orb, ball);
          orb.age = orb.life; // mark for removal
        }
      }
    }
    this.orbs = this.orbs.filter(o => o.age < o.life);

    // tick active effects
    for (const eff of this.activeEffects) eff.timer -= dt;
    this.activeEffects = this.activeEffects.filter(e => e.timer > 0 || e.permanent);
  }
  spawnOrb() {
    const g = this.game;
    const type = choice(POWERUP_TYPES.filter(t => g.settings.enabledPowerups[t.id] !== false));
    if (!type) return;
    const x = rand(g.width * 0.3, g.width * 0.7);
    const y = rand(60, g.height - 60);
    this.orbs.push(new PowerupOrb(g, type, x, y));
  }
  collect(orb, ball) {
    const g = this.game;
    // determine which side "gets" the powerup based on ball's last direction
    const beneficiary = ball.vx > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
    const opponent = beneficiary === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left';
    g.fx.ring(orb.x, orb.y, orb.type.color);
    g.fx.text(orb.x, orb.y - 20, orb.type.label, orb.type.color, 16);
    g.sfx.powerup();
    this.apply(orb.type.id, beneficiary, opponent, ball);
  }
  apply(id, beneficiary, opponent, ball) {
    const g = this.game;
    const bPaddle = g.getPaddleBySide(beneficiary);
    const oPaddle = g.getPaddleBySide(opponent);
    switch (id) {
      case 'multiball':
        g.spawnExtraBalls(2, ball);
        break;
      case 'giant':
        if (bPaddle) bPaddle.giantTimer = 8;
        break;
      case 'shrink':
        if (oPaddle) oPaddle.shrink = 0.5;
        break;
      case 'speedup':
        g.balls.forEach(b => b.normalizeSpeed(Math.min(b.currentSpeed() * 1.5, g.settings.maxBallSpeed)));
        break;
      case 'slowmo':
        g.balls.forEach(b => b.normalizeSpeed(Math.max(b.currentSpeed() * 0.55, 140)));
        break;
      case 'curve':
        ball.curve = (beneficiary === 'left' ? 1 : -1) * 260;
        this.activeEffects.push({ type: 'curve', ball, timer: 6 });
        setTimeout(() => { ball.curve = 0; }, 6000);
        break;
      case 'shield':
        if (bPaddle) bPaddle.shieldHits = (bPaddle.shieldHits || 0) + 1;
        break;
      case 'invert':
        if (oPaddle) oPaddle.inverted = true;
        setTimeout(() => { if (oPaddle) oPaddle.inverted = false; }, 6000);
        break;
      case 'ghost':
        ball.ghost = true;
        setTimeout(() => { ball.ghost = false; }, 5000);
        break;
      case 'magnet':
        if (bPaddle) bPaddle.magnetTimer = 7;
        break;
      case 'gravity':
        const well = { x: g.width / 2, y: g.height / 2, radius: 220, strength: 900 };
        g.balls.forEach(b => b.gravityWells.push(well));
        setTimeout(() => { g.balls.forEach(b => { b.gravityWells = b.gravityWells.filter(w => w !== well); }); }, 5000);
        break;
      case 'stun':
        if (oPaddle) oPaddle.stunTimer = 1.4;
        break;
      case 'tripleball':
        g.spawnExtraBalls(3, ball);
        break;
      case 'megapaddle':
        if (bPaddle) { bPaddle.giantTimer = 6; bPaddle.megaTimer = 6; }
        break;
      case 'mirror': {
        const p1 = g.paddles[0], p2 = g.paddles[1];
        if (p1 && p2) {
          const tmp = p1.y; p1.y = p2.y; p2.y = tmp;
          g.fx.ring(g.width / 2, g.height / 2, '#00bfff');
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'freeze':
        g.balls.forEach(b => b.normalizeSpeed(Math.max(b.currentSpeed() * 0.35, 90)));
        g.fx.flash('#7df9ff', 0.15);
        break;
      case 'blackhole': {
        const hole = { x: g.width / 2, y: g.height / 2, radius: 280, strength: 1800 };
        g.balls.forEach(b => b.gravityWells.push(hole));
        g.fx.ring(g.width / 2, g.height / 2, '#1a0033');
        setTimeout(() => { g.balls.forEach(b => { b.gravityWells = b.gravityWells.filter(w => w !== hole); }); }, 4000);
        break;
      }
      case 'bonus':
        if (bPaddle) {
          bPaddle.score++;
          g.stats.totalPoints++;
          g.fx.text(bPaddle.side === 'left' ? 100 : g.width - 100, g.height / 2, 'BONUS POINT!', '#ffd23f', 22);
          g.sfx.score(true);
        }
        break;
      case 'barrier': {
        const barrier = { x: g.width / 2 - 6, y: 0, w: 12, h: g.height, life: 5, color: '#66ffcc' };
        g.tempBarriers = g.tempBarriers || [];
        g.tempBarriers.push(barrier);
        break;
      }
      case 'chaos_orb': {
        const pool = POWERUP_TYPES.map(t => t.id).filter(pid => pid !== 'chaos_orb');
        this.apply(choice(pool), beneficiary, opponent, ball);
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    for (const o of this.orbs) o.draw(ctx);
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: AI OPPONENT
   ============================================================ */

const AI_PROFILES = {
  rookie:  { reaction: 0.34, error: 46, speedMul: 0.62, predictive: 0.15, maxCorrection: 0.55 },
  amateur: { reaction: 0.20, error: 28, speedMul: 0.78, predictive: 0.45, maxCorrection: 0.72 },
  pro:     { reaction: 0.10, error: 14, speedMul: 0.95, predictive: 0.85, maxCorrection: 0.92 },
  legend:  { reaction: 0.045, error: 5,  speedMul: 1.12, predictive: 1.0, maxCorrection: 1.0 },
};

class AIController {
  constructor(game, paddle, profileKey = 'pro') {
    this.game = game;
    this.paddle = paddle;
    this.setProfile(profileKey);
    this.reactionTimer = 0;
    this.targetY = game.height / 2;
    this.smoothedTargetY = game.height / 2;
    this.errorOffset = 0;
    this.errorTimer = 0;
  }
  setProfile(key) {
    this.profileKey = key;
    this.profile = AI_PROFILES[key] || AI_PROFILES.pro;
  }
  predictBallY(ball) {
    const g = this.game;
    if (ball.vx === 0) return ball.y;
    const paddleX = this.paddle.side === 'left' ? this.paddle.x + this.paddle.width : this.paddle.x;
    const approaching = (this.paddle.side === 'left' && ball.vx < 0) || (this.paddle.side === 'right' && ball.vx > 0);
    if (!approaching) {
      // ball moving away — drift back toward center, lazily track
      return g.height / 2 + (ball.y - g.height / 2) * 0.3;
    }
    let x = ball.x, y = ball.y, vx = ball.vx, vy = ball.vy;
    let iterations = 0;
    while (((vx < 0 && x > paddleX) || (vx > 0 && x < paddleX)) && iterations < 400) {
      const dt = 1 / 120;
      x += vx * dt; y += vy * dt;
      if (y < ball.radius && vy < 0) vy *= -1;
      if (y > g.height - ball.radius && vy > 0) vy *= -1;
      iterations++;
    }
    return y;
  }
  update(dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    const paddle = this.paddle;
    paddle.speed = g.settings.paddleSpeed * this.profile.speedMul;

    // pick nearest relevant ball (approaching this side)
    let target = null, bestT = Infinity;
    for (const ball of g.balls) {
      const approaching = (paddle.side === 'left' && ball.vx < 0) || (paddle.side === 'right' && ball.vx > 0);
      const paddleX = paddle.side === 'left' ? paddle.x : paddle.x;
      const t = Math.abs((ball.x - paddleX) / (ball.vx || 1));
      if (approaching && t < bestT) { bestT = t; target = ball; }
    }
    if (!target) target = g.balls[0];
    if (!target) return;

    this.reactionTimer -= dt;
    this.errorTimer -= dt;
    if (this.errorTimer <= 0) {
      this.errorOffset = rand(-this.profile.error, this.profile.error);
      this.errorTimer = rand(0.4, 1.1);
    }

    if (this.reactionTimer <= 0) {
      const predicted = this.predictBallY(target) * this.profile.predictive +
                         target.y * (1 - this.profile.predictive);
      this.targetY = predicted + this.errorOffset;
      this.reactionTimer = this.profile.reaction;
    }

    if (paddle.inverted) {
      const center = g.height / 2;
      this.targetY = center - (this.targetY - center);
    }

    // Smoothly chase the (possibly step-updated) target so the AI's aim point
    // itself doesn't teleport — this is on top of the velocity smoothing below,
    // and further removes any remaining jitter from discrete reaction updates.
    const targetSmoothing = 1 - Math.pow(0.0005, dt);
    this.smoothedTargetY = lerp(this.smoothedTargetY, this.targetY, targetSmoothing);

    const currentCenter = paddle.centerY();
    let diff = this.smoothedTargetY - currentCenter;
    diff = clamp(diff, -paddle.effectiveHeight() * this.profile.maxCorrection, paddle.effectiveHeight() * this.profile.maxCorrection);

    // Smooth, proportional approach instead of full-speed-then-hard-stop:
    // ease the paddle's velocity based on distance to target so it decelerates
    // naturally instead of snapping, which is what reads as "choppy" movement.
    const deadzone = 4;
    const easeRadius = 46; // px within which the paddle starts slowing down
    const absDiff = Math.abs(diff);
    let desiredSpeed = 0;
    if (absDiff > deadzone) {
      const dir = Math.sign(diff);
      const speedScale = absDiff < easeRadius ? clamp(absDiff / easeRadius, 0.12, 1) : 1;
      const roleSpeedMul = (paddle.roleController && paddle.roleController.role.passive.speedMul) || 1;
      desiredSpeed = dir * paddle.speed * speedScale * (paddle.dashMul || 1) * roleSpeedMul;
    }
    // smoothly interpolate current velocity toward the desired velocity rather
    // than snapping instantly, so direction changes and stops feel continuous
    const smoothing = 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt); // frame-rate independent lerp factor
    paddle.vy = lerp(paddle.vy, desiredSpeed, smoothing);
    if (Math.abs(paddle.vy) < 2) paddle.vy = 0;
    if (paddle.vy !== 0) {
      const h = paddle.effectiveHeight();
      if (paddle.stunTimer <= 0) {
        paddle.y = clamp(paddle.y + paddle.vy * dt, 0, g.height - h);
      }
    }
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: CREATIVE / DRAW MODE
   Players draw a shape which becomes their collidable paddle.
   The drawn stroke is sampled into a set of collision segments
   that the ball bounces off of, and rendered as a glowing line.
   ============================================================ */

class DrawnPaddle {
  constructor(game, side, color) {
    this.game = game;
    this.side = side;
    this.color = color;
    this.points = []; // raw drawn points {x,y} in local paddle space (relative to origin)
    this.originX = side === 'left' ? game.width * 0.16 : game.width * 0.84;
    this.originY = game.height / 2;
    this.offsetY = 0; // vertical slide input, drawn shape stays same, moves as unit
    this.speed = game.settings.paddleSpeed;
    this.score = 0;
    this.segments = [];
    this.bounds = { minY: -60, maxY: 60 };
    this.isDrawing = false;
    this.hasShape = false;
    this.thickness = 6;
  }
  clear() {
    this.points = [];
    this.segments = [];
    this.hasShape = false;
  }
  addPoint(x, y) {
    // store relative to origin so movement translates the whole shape
    this.points.push({ x: x - this.originX, y: y - this.originY });
    this.rebuildSegments();
  }
  rebuildSegments() {
    this.segments = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < this.points.length - 1; i++) {
      this.segments.push([this.points[i], this.points[i + 1]]);
    }
    if (this.points.length > 1) {
      this.hasShape = true;
      let minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
      for (const p of this.points) { minY = Math.min(minY, p.y); maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y); }
      this.bounds = { minY, maxY };
    }
  }
  finishDrawing() {
    this.isDrawing = false;
    // smooth the points slightly (moving average) for nicer collision + visuals
    if (this.points.length > 4) {
      const smoothed = [this.points[0]];
      for (let i = 1; i < this.points.length - 1; i++) {
        smoothed.push({
          x: (this.points[i - 1].x + this.points[i].x + this.points[i + 1].x) / 3,
          y: (this.points[i - 1].y + this.points[i].y + this.points[i + 1].y) / 3,
        });
      }
      smoothed.push(this.points[this.points.length - 1]);
      this.points = smoothed;
      this.rebuildSegments();
    }
  }
  moveBy(dy, dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    const margin = 40;
    this.offsetY = clamp(this.offsetY + dy * this.speed * dt,
      margin - this.bounds.minY - this.originY < 0 ? -this.originY - this.bounds.minY + margin : -1e9,
      g.height - margin - this.originY - this.bounds.maxY);
    // simpler clamp fallback
    const top = -this.originY - this.bounds.minY + margin;
    const bottom = g.height - margin - this.originY - this.bounds.maxY;
    this.offsetY = clamp(this.offsetY, Math.min(top, bottom), Math.max(top, bottom));
  }
  worldSegments() {
    const oy = this.originY + this.offsetY;
    return this.segments.map(([a, b]) => ([
      { x: a.x + this.originX, y: a.y + oy },
      { x: b.x + this.originX, y: b.y + oy },
    ]));
  }
  // closest point on segment to circle center, for collision
  static closestPointOnSegment(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by) {
    const abx = bx - ax, aby = by - ay;
    const apx = px - ax, apy = py - ay;
    const lenSq = abx * abx + aby * aby || 1;
    let t = (apx * abx + apy * aby) / lenSq;
    t = clamp(t, 0, 1);
    return { x: ax + abx * t, y: ay + aby * t };
  }
  collideBall(ball) {
    const segs = this.worldSegments();
    for (const [a, b] of segs) {
      const cp = DrawnPaddle.closestPointOnSegment(ball.x, ball.y, a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y);
      const dx = ball.x - cp.x, dy = ball.y - cp.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < ball.radius + this.thickness / 2) {
        const overlap = (ball.radius + this.thickness / 2) - d;
        const nx = dx / (d || 1), ny = dy / (d || 1);
        ball.x += nx * overlap;
        ball.y += ny * overlap;
        // reflect velocity around segment normal
        const dot = ball.vx * nx + ball.vy * ny;
        ball.vx = ball.vx - 2 * dot * nx;
        ball.vy = ball.vy - 2 * dot * ny;
        let speed = ball.currentSpeed();
        speed = Math.min(speed * 1.04, ball.game.settings.maxBallSpeed);
        ball.normalizeSpeed(speed);
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    const g = this.game;
    const segs = this.worldSegments();
    if (segs.length === 0) {
      // placeholder hint
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba(this.color, 0.4);
      ctx.setLineDash([6, 8]);
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(this.originX - 50, this.originY - 60, 100, 120);
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(this.color, 0.7);
      ctx.font = '600 13px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('DRAW HERE', this.originX, this.originY);
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }
    ctx.save();
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 20 * g.settings.glowIntensity;
    ctx.strokeStyle = this.color;
    ctx.lineWidth = this.thickness;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(segs[0][0].x, segs[0][0].y);
    for (const [, b] of segs) ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/* Manages the drawing surface UI/input for creative mode */
class CreativeModeController {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.left = new DrawnPaddle(game, 'left', '#ff00d4');
    this.right = new DrawnPaddle(game, 'right', '#00f0ff');
    this.activeSide = null; // which side is currently being drawn on by mouse/touch
  }
  reset() {
    this.left.clear();
    this.right.clear();
  }
  sideForX(x) {
    return x < this.game.width / 2 ? 'left' : 'right';
  }
  pointerDown(x, y) {
    const side = this.sideForX(x);
    const paddle = side === 'left' ? this.left : this.right;
    paddle.clear();
    paddle.isDrawing = true;
    paddle.originX = side === 'left' ? this.game.width * 0.16 : this.game.width * 0.84;
    paddle.originY = this.game.height / 2;
    paddle.offsetY = 0;
    paddle.addPoint(x, y);
    this.activeSide = side;
  }
  pointerMove(x, y) {
    if (!this.activeSide) return;
    const paddle = this.activeSide === 'left' ? this.left : this.right;
    if (!paddle.isDrawing) return;
    const last = paddle.points[paddle.points.length - 1];
    if (last) {
      const lastWorld = { x: last.x + paddle.originX, y: last.y + paddle.originY };
      if (dist(x, y, lastWorld.x, lastWorld.y) < 4) return; // skip tiny movements
    }
    paddle.addPoint(x, y);
  }
  pointerUp() {
    if (!this.activeSide) return;
    const paddle = this.activeSide === 'left' ? this.left : this.right;
    paddle.finishDrawing();
    this.activeSide = null;
  }
  update(dt) {
    // nothing continuous needed beyond input-driven moveBy calls from main loop
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    this.left.draw(ctx);
    this.right.draw(ctx);
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: ARENA OBSTACLES
   Bumpers (circular deflectors), portals (teleport pairs),
   wind zones (constant force regions), brick walls (breakable)
   ============================================================ */

class Bumper {
  constructor(x, y, radius, color) {
    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.radius = radius;
    this.color = color || '#ff8800';
    this.pulse = 0;
  }
  update(dt) { if (this.pulse > 0) this.pulse -= dt * 3; }
  collide(ball, fx, sfx) {
    const d = dist(ball.x, ball.y, this.x, this.y);
    if (d < this.radius + ball.radius) {
      const nx = (ball.x - this.x) / (d || 1), ny = (ball.y - this.y) / (d || 1);
      const overlap = (this.radius + ball.radius) - d;
      ball.x += nx * overlap; ball.y += ny * overlap;
      const dot = ball.vx * nx + ball.vy * ny;
      ball.vx = (ball.vx - 2 * dot * nx) * 1.12;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy - 2 * dot * ny) * 1.12;
      ball.normalizeSpeed(Math.min(ball.currentSpeed(), ball.game.settings.maxBallSpeed));
      this.pulse = 1;
      fx.ring(this.x, this.y, this.color);
      fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, this.color, 10, { maxSpeed: 160 });
      sfx.tone(500, 0.08, 'square', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 900 });
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    ctx.save();
    const glow = 18 + this.pulse * 20;
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = glow;
    ctx.strokeStyle = this.color;
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, this.radius + this.pulse * 4, 0, TAU);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(this.color, 0.12 + this.pulse * 0.15);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

class PortalPair {
  constructor(ax, ay, bx, by, color) {
    this.a = { x: ax, y: ay };
    this.b = { x: bx, y: by };
    this.radius = 22;
    this.color = color || '#c400ff';
    this.cooldownA = 0;
    this.cooldownB = 0;
    this.phase = 0;
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.phase += dt;
    if (this.cooldownA > 0) this.cooldownA -= dt;
    if (this.cooldownB > 0) this.cooldownB -= dt;
  }
  collide(ball) {
    if (this.cooldownA <= 0 && dist(ball.x, ball.y, this.a.x, this.a.y) < this.radius) {
      ball.x = this.b.x; ball.y = this.b.y;
      this.cooldownB = 0.5;
      return this.color;
    }
    if (this.cooldownB <= 0 && dist(ball.x, ball.y, this.b.x, this.b.y) < this.radius) {
      ball.x = this.a.x; ball.y = this.a.y;
      this.cooldownA = 0.5;
      return this.color;
    }
    return null;
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    for (const p of [this.a, this.b]) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.rotate(this.phase * 2);
      ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
      ctx.strokeStyle = this.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.setLineDash([8, 6]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(0, 0, this.radius, 0, TAU);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }
}

class WindZone {
  constructor(x, y, w, h, fx_, fy_, color) {
    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.w = w; this.h = h;
    this.fx = fx_; this.fy = fy_;
    this.color = color || '#00ffaa';
    this.particles = [];
    this.spawnTimer = 0;
  }
  contains(px, py) { return px > this.x && px < this.x + this.w && py > this.y && py < this.y + this.h; }
  applyForce(ball, dt) {
    if (this.contains(ball.x, ball.y)) {
      ball.vx += this.fx * dt;
      ball.vy += this.fy * dt;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.spawnTimer -= dt;
    if (this.spawnTimer <= 0) {
      this.spawnTimer = 0.15;
      this.particles.push({
        x: rand(this.x, this.x + this.w), y: rand(this.y, this.y + this.h),
        vx: this.fx * 0.15, vy: this.fy * 0.15, life: 1.2,
      });
    }
    this.particles.forEach(p => { p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.life -= dt; });
    this.particles = this.particles.filter(p => p.life > 0 && this.contains(p.x, p.y));
  }
  draw(ctx) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(this.color, 0.04);
    ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba(this.color, 0.25);
    ctx.setLineDash([4, 6]);
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
    ctx.strokeRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    for (const p of this.particles) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = p.life;
      ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 2, 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

class Brick {
  constructor(x, y, w, h, hp, color) {
    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.w = w; this.h = h;
    this.hp = hp; this.maxHp = hp;
    this.color = color || '#00f0ff';
    this.alive = true;
    this.shake = 0;
  }
  collide(ball, fx, sfx) {
    if (!this.alive) return false;
    if (circleRectCollide(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h)) {
      const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
      const dx = (ball.x - cx) / (this.w / 2), dy = (ball.y - cy) / (this.h / 2);
      if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) ball.vx *= -1; else ball.vy *= -1;
      this.hp--;
      this.shake = 1;
      fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, this.color, 8, { maxSpeed: 140 });
      sfx.tone(300 + this.hp * 100, 0.06, 'square', { gain: 0.15 });
      if (this.hp <= 0) {
        this.alive = false;
        fx.ring(cx, cy, this.color);
        sfx.explosion();
      }
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  update(dt) { if (this.shake > 0) this.shake -= dt * 5; }
  draw(ctx) {
    if (!this.alive) return;
    const sx = this.shake > 0 ? rand(-2, 2) : 0;
    const alpha = 0.3 + (this.hp / this.maxHp) * 0.7;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.shadowColor = this.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(this.color, alpha * 0.25);
    ctx.strokeStyle = this.color;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    roundRect(ctx, this.x + sx, this.y, this.w, this.h, 4);
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/* Manager that assembles a layout per-arena-preset */
class ObstacleManager {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.bumpers = [];
    this.portals = [];
    this.windZones = [];
    this.bricks = [];
    this.layout = 'none';
  }
  clear() {
    this.bumpers = []; this.portals = []; this.windZones = []; this.bricks = [];
  }
  setLayout(name) {
    const g = this.game;
    this.clear();
    this.layout = name;
    const w = g.width, h = g.height;
    if (name === 'none') return;
    if (name === 'bumpers') {
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2, h / 2, 34, '#ff8800'));
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2 - 120, h / 2 - 100, 22, '#ff00d4'));
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2 + 120, h / 2 + 100, 22, '#00f0ff'));
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2 - 120, h / 2 + 100, 22, '#ffe600'));
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2 + 120, h / 2 - 100, 22, '#00ff88'));
    } else if (name === 'portals') {
      this.portals.push(new PortalPair(w * 0.3, h * 0.25, w * 0.7, h * 0.75, '#c400ff'));
      this.portals.push(new PortalPair(w * 0.3, h * 0.75, w * 0.7, h * 0.25, '#00fff0'));
    } else if (name === 'wind') {
      this.windZones.push(new WindZone(w / 2 - 80, 0, 160, h * 0.4, 0, 260, '#00ffaa'));
      this.windZones.push(new WindZone(w / 2 - 80, h * 0.6, 160, h * 0.4, 0, -260, '#ff6699'));
    } else if (name === 'bricks') {
      const cols = 6, rows = 4;
      const bw = 50, bh = 18, gap = 6;
      const startX = w / 2 - (cols * (bw + gap)) / 2;
      const startY = h / 2 - (rows * (bh + gap)) / 2;
      const colors = ['#ff00d4', '#00f0ff', '#ffe600', '#00ff88'];
      for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
          this.bricks.push(new Brick(
            startX + c * (bw + gap), startY + r * (bh + gap), bw, bh,
            2 + (rows - r) % 2, colors[r % colors.length]
          ));
        }
      }
    } else if (name === 'gauntlet') {
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2, h * 0.2, 26, '#ff8800'));
      this.bumpers.push(new Bumper(w / 2, h * 0.8, 26, '#ff8800'));
      this.windZones.push(new WindZone(w / 2 - 60, h * 0.42, 120, h * 0.16, rand(-80, 80), 0, '#00ffaa'));
      this.portals.push(new PortalPair(w * 0.15, h * 0.5, w * 0.85, h * 0.5, '#c400ff'));
    }
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.bumpers.forEach(b => b.update(dt));
    this.portals.forEach(p => p.update(dt));
    this.windZones.forEach(wz => wz.update(dt));
    this.bricks.forEach(b => b.update(dt));
    this.bricks = this.bricks.filter(b => b.alive || b.shake > -1);
  }
  resolveBall(ball) {
    const g = this.game;
    for (const b of this.bumpers) b.collide(ball, g.fx, g.sfx);
    for (const p of this.portals) {
      const c = p.collide(ball);
      if (c) { g.fx.ring(ball.x, ball.y, c); g.sfx.tone(700, 0.1, 'sine', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 1200 }); }
    }
    for (const wz of this.windZones) wz.applyForce(ball, 1 / 60);
    for (const br of this.bricks) br.collide(ball, g.fx, g.sfx);
  }
  allBricksCleared() { return this.layout === 'bricks' && this.bricks.every(b => !b.alive); }
  draw(ctx) {
    this.windZones.forEach(wz => wz.draw(ctx));
    this.bricks.forEach(b => b.draw(ctx));
    this.bumpers.forEach(b => b.draw(ctx));
    this.portals.forEach(p => p.draw(ctx));
  }
}

  { id: 'bumpers', label: 'Bumper Field', desc: 'Pinball-style deflectors' },

  { id: 'bricks', label: 'Brick Breaker', desc: 'Smash the center wall' },
  { id: 'gauntlet', label: 'Gauntlet', desc: 'Bumpers, wind, and a portal' },
];
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: ACHIEVEMENTS
   ============================================================ */

const ACHIEVEMENTS = [
  { id: 'first_win', name: 'First Blood', desc: 'Win your first match', icon: '🏅', check: (s) => s.wins >= 1 },
  { id: 'ten_wins', name: 'Regular', desc: 'Win 10 matches', icon: '🎖️', check: (s) => s.wins >= 10 },

  { id: 'rally_50', name: 'Unbreakable', desc: 'Hit a 50+ shot rally', icon: '💎', check: (s) => s.bestRally >= 50 },
  { id: 'tournament_champ', name: 'Champion', desc: 'Win a full tournament', icon: '👑', check: (s) => s.tournamentWins >= 1 },
  { id: 'survival_60', name: 'Last Stand', desc: 'Survive 60 seconds', icon: '🛡️', check: (s) => s.survivalBest >= 60 },
  { id: 'survival_120', name: 'Immortal', desc: 'Survive 120 seconds', icon: '⚡', check: (s) => s.survivalBest >= 120 },

  { id: 'chaos_survivor', name: 'Chaos Agent', desc: 'Win a match in Chaos Mode', icon: '🌀', check: (s) => s.chaosWins >= 1 },
  { id: 'boss_defeated', name: 'Boss Slayer', desc: 'Defeat the Boss Rush', icon: '🐲', check: (s) => s.bossDefeated },
  { id: 'games_100', name: 'Dedicated', desc: 'Play 100 games', icon: '📈', check: (s) => s.gamesPlayed >= 100 },
];

class AchievementManager {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.unlocked = this.load();
    this.queue = [];
    this.toastTimer = 0;
    this.currentToast = null;
  }
  load() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('neonpong_achievements');
      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
    return {};
  }
  save() {
    try { localStorage.setItem('neonpong_achievements', JSON.stringify(this.unlocked)); } catch (e) {}
  }
  checkAll() {
    const s = this.game.stats;
    for (const a of ACHIEVEMENTS) {
      if (!this.unlocked[a.id] && a.check(s)) {
        this.unlocked[a.id] = Date.now();
        this.queue.push(a);
      }
    }
    if (this.queue.length) this.save();
  }
  update(dt) {
    if (this.currentToast) {
      this.toastTimer -= dt;
      if (this.toastTimer <= 0) this.currentToast = null;
    }
    if (!this.currentToast && this.queue.length) {
      this.currentToast = this.queue.shift();
      this.toastTimer = 3.2;
      this.game.sfx.powerup();
    }
  }
  progress() {
    return { unlocked: Object.keys(this.unlocked).length, total: ACHIEVEMENTS.length };
  }
  drawToast(ctx, w) {
    if (!this.currentToast) return;
    const a = this.currentToast;
    const slideIn = clamp(1 - this.toastTimer / 3.2, 0, 1);
    const enter = Math.min(1, slideIn * 6);
    const exit = this.toastTimer < 0.4 ? this.toastTimer / 0.4 : 1;
    const alpha = Math.min(enter, exit);
    const y = 16 + (1 - Math.min(enter, 1)) * -20;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    const boxW = 300, boxH = 56;
    const x = w / 2 - boxW / 2;
    ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd700';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,5,0,0.88)';
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    roundRect(ctx, x, y, boxW, boxH, 10);
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.font = '28px sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText(a.icon, x + 14, y + boxH / 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.font = '700 13px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('ACHIEVEMENT UNLOCKED', x + 58, y + 18);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.font = '600 15px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(a.name, x + 58, y + 38);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: BOSS RUSH
   A single escalating AI boss with telegraphed special abilities
   that trigger at HP thresholds. Player has 5 lives (hits to concede).
   ============================================================ */

const BOSS_PHASES = [
  { hpThreshold: 1.0, name: 'AWAKENING', color: '#ff3300', speedMul: 0.9, ability: null },
  { hpThreshold: 0.7, name: 'ACCELERATION', color: '#ff8800', speedMul: 1.1, ability: 'speedburst' },
  { hpThreshold: 0.4, name: 'FRACTURE', color: '#c400ff', speedMul: 1.2, ability: 'multiball' },
  { hpThreshold: 0.15, name: 'DESPERATION', color: '#ff0044', speedMul: 1.4, ability: 'giant' },
];

class BossController {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.maxHp = 15;
    this.hp = this.maxHp;
    this.phaseIndex = 0;
    this.abilityTimer = rand(4, 6);
    this.telegraphTimer = 0;
    this.telegraphActive = null;
    this.playerLives = 5;
    this.ai = null;
  }
  setup() {
    const g = this.game;
    this.hp = this.maxHp;
    this.phaseIndex = 0;
    this.playerLives = 5;
    g.paddles = [
      new Paddle(g, 'left', { color: '#00f0ff', height: 100 }),
      new Paddle(g, 'right', { color: BOSS_PHASES[0].color, height: 130 }),
    ];
    this.ai = new AIController(g, g.paddles[1], 'pro');
    this.ai.profile = Object.assign({}, AI_PROFILES.pro);
    g.obstacles.setLayout('none');
  }
  currentPhase() { return BOSS_PHASES[this.phaseIndex]; }
  onPlayerScore() {
    this.hp -= 1;
    const g = this.game;
    const ratio = this.hp / this.maxHp;
    g.fx.flash(this.currentPhase().color, 0.2);
    g.fx.shake(6, 4);
    // advance phase if threshold crossed
    for (let i = BOSS_PHASES.length - 1; i > this.phaseIndex; i--) {
      if (ratio <= BOSS_PHASES[i].hpThreshold) {
        this.phaseIndex = i;
        const phase = BOSS_PHASES[i];
        g.paddles[1].color = phase.color;
        this.ai.profile.speedMul = AI_PROFILES.pro.speedMul * phase.speedMul;
        g.fx.text(g.width / 2, g.height / 2 - 100, 'PHASE: ' + phase.name, phase.color, 26);
        g.sfx.explosion();
        break;
      }
    }
    return this.hp <= 0;
  }
  onBossScore() {
    this.playerLives -= 1;
    return this.playerLives <= 0;
  }
  update(dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    this.abilityTimer -= dt;
    if (this.abilityTimer <= 0 && this.currentPhase().ability) {
      this.triggerAbility(this.currentPhase().ability);
      this.abilityTimer = rand(5, 8);
    }
  }
  triggerAbility(ability) {
    const g = this.game;
    const boss = g.paddles[1];
    g.fx.text(g.width - 120, 60, ability.toUpperCase() + '!', boss.color, 18);
    if (ability === 'speedburst') {
      g.balls.forEach(b => b.normalizeSpeed(Math.min(b.currentSpeed() * 1.6, g.settings.maxBallSpeed)));
      g.sfx.tone(200, 0.3, 'sawtooth', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 600 });
    } else if (ability === 'multiball' && g.balls[0]) {
      g.spawnExtraBalls(1, g.balls[0]);
      g.sfx.powerup();
    } else if (ability === 'giant') {
      boss.giantTimer = 5;
      g.sfx.tone(100, 0.4, 'square', { gain: 0.25 });
    }
  }
  hpRatio() { return Math.max(0, this.hp / this.maxHp); }
  drawHud(ctx, w) {
    ctx.save();
    const barW = 300, barH = 16;
    const x = w / 2 - barW / 2, y = 20;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
    roundRect(ctx, x, y, barW, barH, 8); ctx.fill();
    const phase = this.currentPhase();
    ctx.fillStyle = phase.color;
    ctx.shadowColor = phase.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
    roundRect(ctx, x, y, barW * this.hpRatio(), barH, 8); ctx.fill();
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    roundRect(ctx, x, y, barW, barH, 8); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = '700 11px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('BOSS — ' + phase.name, w / 2, y - 6);
    ctx.font = '600 13px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('♥'.repeat(Math.max(0, this.playerLives)), w / 2, y + barH + 18);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: PADDLE SKINS
   Cosmetic shape variants for standard (non-creative) modes.
   Each skin defines a custom draw routine; collision box stays
   the same rectangle underneath for fairness.
   ============================================================ */

const PADDLE_SKINS = {
  classic: {
    label: 'Classic Bar',
    draw(ctx, p, r, glowMul) {
      ctx.shadowBlur = 18 * glowMul * (p.stunTimer > 0 ? 0.3 : 1);
      ctx.fillStyle = p.stunTimer > 0 ? hexToRgba(p.color, 0.35) : p.color;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  },
  segmented: {
    label: 'Segmented',
    draw(ctx, p, r, glowMul) {
      const segs = 5;
      const gap = 4;
      const segH = (r.h - gap * (segs - 1)) / segs;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 16 * glowMul;
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
      for (let i = 0; i < segs; i++) {
        roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y + i * (segH + gap), r.w, segH, 3);
        ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  },
  diamond: {
    label: 'Diamond Chain',
    draw(ctx, p, r, glowMul) {
      const count = 6;
      const dH = r.h / count;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 16 * glowMul;
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const cy = r.y + dH * i + dH / 2;
        const cx = r.x + r.w / 2;
        const s = r.w / 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - dH / 2 + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(cx + s, cy);
        ctx.lineTo(cx, cy + dH / 2 - 2);
        ctx.lineTo(cx - s, cy);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  },
  energy: {
    label: 'Energy Blade',
    draw(ctx, p, r, glowMul) {
      ctx.shadowBlur = 26 * glowMul;
      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(r.x, r.y, r.x, r.y + r.h);
      grad.addColorStop(0, hexToRgba(p.color, 0.2));
      grad.addColorStop(0.5, p.color);
      grad.addColorStop(1, hexToRgba(p.color, 0.2));
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, r.w / 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.6;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x + r.w * 0.3, r.y + 4, r.w * 0.4, r.h - 8, r.w / 4);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
  },
  brick: {
    label: 'Brick Wall',
    draw(ctx, p, r, glowMul) {
      ctx.shadowBlur = 12 * glowMul;
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      const rowH = 10;
      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y += rowH) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x, y); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w, y); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
  },
  pulse: {
    label: 'Pulse Core',
    draw(ctx, p, r, glowMul) {
      const t = performance.now() / 400;
      const pulse = 0.7 + Math.sin(t) * 0.3;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 24 * glowMul * pulse;
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 6);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba('#ffffff', 0.5 * pulse);
      roundRect(ctx, r.x + 2, r.y + r.h * 0.3, r.w - 4, r.h * 0.4, 4);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  },
};

function drawPaddleWithSkin(ctx, game, paddle) {
  const r = paddle.rect();
  const role = (game.settings.rolesEnabled && paddle.roleController) ? paddle.roleController.role : null;

  // When a role is equipped, its own bespoke visual fully replaces the
  // cosmetic skin — the role IS the paddle's appearance, not an overlay
  // on top of a separately-chosen skin.
  if (role && ROLE_VISUALS[role.id]) {
    drawRolePaddle(ctx, game, paddle, r);
    return;
  }

  const skinKey = paddle.side === 'left' ? game.settings.skinP1 : game.settings.skinP2;
  const skin = PADDLE_SKINS[skinKey] || PADDLE_SKINS.classic;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor = paddle.color;
  skin.draw(ctx, paddle, r, game.settings.glowIntensity);
  if (paddle.giantTimer > 0) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba('#ffffff', 0.6);
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    roundRect(ctx, r.x - 3, r.y - 3, r.w + 6, r.h + 6, 7);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
/* Custom hue-based color picker helper: converts HSL slider to hex */
function hslToHex(h, s, l) {
  s /= 100; l /= 100;
  const k = n => (n + h / 30) % 12;
  const a = s * Math.min(l, 1 - l);
  const f = n => l - a * Math.max(-1, Math.min(k(n) - 3, Math.min(9 - k(n), 1)));
  const toHex = x => Math.round(255 * x).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  return `#${toHex(f(0))}${toHex(f(8))}${toHex(f(4))}`;
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: GHOST REPLAY + TIME ATTACK
   Records left-paddle Y positions during a rally and can replay
   them as a faint translucent "ghost" paddle for comparison runs.
   ============================================================ */

class GhostRecorder {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.recording = [];
    this.isRecording = false;
    this.bestRun = null; // {frames: [...], score}
    this.playback = null;
    this.playbackIndex = 0;
  }
  startRecording() {
    this.recording = [];
    this.isRecording = true;
  }
  sample(paddleY) {
    if (this.isRecording) this.recording.push(paddleY);
  }
  stopRecording(rallyLength) {
    this.isRecording = false;
    if (!this.bestRun || rallyLength > this.bestRun.score) {
      this.bestRun = { frames: this.recording.slice(), score: rallyLength };
      try {
        localStorage.setItem('neonpong_ghost', JSON.stringify(this.bestRun));
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  loadBest() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('neonpong_ghost');
      if (raw) this.bestRun = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  startPlayback() {
    this.loadBest();
    if (this.bestRun) { this.playback = this.bestRun.frames; this.playbackIndex = 0; }
  }
  stopPlayback() { this.playback = null; }
  currentGhostY() {
    if (!this.playback || this.playback.length === 0) return null;
    const y = this.playback[this.playbackIndex % this.playback.length];
    this.playbackIndex++;
    return y;
  }
  draw(ctx, game) {
    if (!this.playback) return;
    const y = this.playback[this.playbackIndex % this.playback.length];
    const h = 40 + (game.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25;
    ctx.shadowColor = '#ffffff';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    roundRect(ctx, 28, y, 14, h, 5);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/* Time Attack: score as many points as possible before the clock runs out */
class TimeAttackController {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.duration = 60;
    this.remaining = 60;
    this.active = false;
  }
  start(duration = 60) {
    this.duration = duration;
    this.remaining = duration;
    this.active = true;
  }
  update(dt) {
    if (!this.active) return false;
    this.remaining -= dt;
    if (this.remaining <= 0) {
      this.remaining = 0;
      this.active = false;
      return true; // signal time-up
    }
    return false;
  }
  drawHud(ctx, w) {
    if (!this.active && this.remaining <= 0) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '800 22px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    const urgent = this.remaining < 10;
    ctx.fillStyle = urgent ? '#ff2266' : '#ffffff';
    ctx.shadowColor = ctx.fillStyle;
    ctx.shadowBlur = urgent ? 20 : 10;
    const s = Math.ceil(this.remaining);
    ctx.fillText(`${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`, w / 2, 110);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: ACCESSIBILITY + MATCH HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

const COLORBLIND_PALETTE = {
  cyber: { p1: '#0072B2', p2: '#E69F00', ball: '#ffffff', bg1: '#03060a', bg2: '#070d16', accent: '#0072B2' },
};

function getEffectiveTheme(game) {
  const th = THEMES[game.settings.theme] || THEMES.cyber;
  if (game.settings.colorblindMode) {
    return Object.assign({}, th, COLORBLIND_PALETTE.cyber, { name: th.name + ' (Colorblind-safe)' });
  }
  return th;
}

class MatchHistory {
  constructor() {
    this.entries = this.load();
  }
  load() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('neonpong_history');
      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
    return [];
  }
  save() {
    try {
      if (this.entries.length > 50) this.entries = this.entries.slice(-50);
      localStorage.setItem('neonpong_history', JSON.stringify(this.entries));
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  log(entry) {
    this.entries.push(Object.assign({ time: Date.now() }, entry));
    this.save();
  }
  recent(n = 10) {
    return this.entries.slice(-n).reverse();
  }
  clear() {
    this.entries = [];
    try { localStorage.removeItem('neonpong_history'); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

function formatHistoryEntry(e) {
  const date = new Date(e.time);
  const dateStr = date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
  const timeStr = date.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  let result = '';
  if (e.mode === 'classic') result = `vs AI (${e.difficulty}) — ${e.won ? 'Won' : 'Lost'} ${e.scoreA}-${e.scoreB}`;
  else if (e.mode === 'twoplayer') result = `2P — ${e.winner} won ${e.scoreA}-${e.scoreB}`;
  else if (e.mode === 'tournament') result = e.won ? 'Tournament — Champion!' : `Tournament — fell round ${e.round}/4`;
  else if (e.mode === 'survival') result = `Survival — ${e.time}s, best rally ${e.rally}`;
  else if (e.mode === 'boss') result = e.won ? 'Boss Rush — Defeated!' : 'Boss Rush — Fell in battle';
  else if (e.mode === 'timeattack') result = `Time Attack — ${e.score} points`;
  else result = e.mode;
  return { dateStr, timeStr, result };
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: PADDLE ROLES — FULL 66-ROLE SYSTEM
   30 Common / 17 Rare / 10 Epic / 6 Legendary / 3 Mythic
   Every single role has its own distinct active ability, bound
   to X (P1) / Left Arrow (P2), auto-usable by CPU via
   aiShouldUse(). Many also carry a small passive flavor stat.
   ============================================================ */

const ROLE_RARITY = {
  common:    { label: 'COMMON',    color: '#9fb4c7', weight: 100 },
  rare:      { label: 'RARE',      color: '#3aa9ff', weight: 32 },
  epic:      { label: 'EPIC',      color: '#c400ff', weight: 11 },
  legendary: { label: 'LEGENDARY', color: '#ffd23f', weight: 3.2 },
  mythic:    { label: 'MYTHIC',    color: '#ff2b4d', weight: 0.6 },
};

function approaching(paddle, ball) {
  return !!ball && ((paddle.side === 'left' && ball.vx < 0) || (paddle.side === 'right' && ball.vx > 0));
}
function ballNear(paddle, ball, dist_ = 70) {
  return !!ball && Math.abs(ball.x - paddle.x) < dist_;
}
function opponentOf(game, paddle) {
  return game.getPaddleBySide(paddle.side === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left');
}
function farBallDeflect(p, ball) {
  return approaching(p, ball) && Math.abs(ball.y - p.centerY()) > 80;
}
function noop() {}
function neverAi() { return false; }

/* ============================================================
   MYTHIC — 3. Demon's bonus is unique among all 67 roles: it
   fires a random OTHER role's primary ability every activation,
   drawn from the full roster — this is how Demon "has every
   ability in the game," rather than one fixed borrowed power.
   ============================================================ */
const MYTHIC_ROLES = [
  { id: 'demon', name: 'Demon', icon: '▲', moveName: 'Demon Mode', desc: 'Transforms the paddle and massively powers up the next shot.',
