function tagRarity(list, rarity) { return list.map(r => Object.assign({ rarity }, r)); }

const PADDLE_ROLES = [
  ...tagRarity(COMMON_ROLES, 'common'),
  ...tagRarity(RARE_ROLES, 'rare'),
  ...tagRarity(EPIC_ROLES, 'epic'),
  ...tagRarity(LEGENDARY_ROLES, 'legendary'),
  ...tagRarity(MYTHIC_ROLES, 'mythic'),
].map(r => Object.assign({
  cooldown: r.cooldown !== undefined ? r.cooldown : 0,
  duration: r.duration || 0,
  activate: r.activate || noop,
  aiShouldUse: r.aiShouldUse || neverAi,
  passive: r.passive || {},
}, r));

function pickWeightedRole(pool) {
  const totalWeight = pool.reduce((sum, r) => sum + ROLE_RARITY[r.rarity].weight, 0);
  let roll = Math.random() * totalWeight;
  for (const role of pool) {
    roll -= ROLE_RARITY[role.rarity].weight;
    if (roll <= 0) return role;
  }
  return pool[pool.length - 1];
}
function getRole(id) { return PADDLE_ROLES.find(r => r.id === id) || PADDLE_ROLES[0]; }

const CHARGE_GATE_INFO = {
  solar: { key: 'solarCharge', target: 5 },
  energy: { key: 'energyCharge', target: 6 },
  reaper: { key: 'reaperCharge', target: 7 },
  demon: { key: 'demonCharge', target: 8 },
  berserker: { key: 'berserkStacks', target: 1 },
};

class RoleController {
  constructor(paddle) {
    this.paddle = paddle;
    this.role = PADDLE_ROLES[0];
    this.cooldownRemaining = 0;
    this.bonusCooldownRemaining = 0;
    paddle.roleController = this;
  }
  assign(role) {
    this.role = role;
    this.cooldownRemaining = 0;
    this.bonusCooldownRemaining = 0;
    const p = this.paddle;
    p.solarCharge = 0; p.energyCharge = 0; p.reaperCharge = 0; p.demonCharge = 0;
    p.berserkStacks = 0; p.guardCharge = 0; p.angelReady = false; p.lunarPhase = false;
    p.infernoActive = 0; p.chargeReady = false; p.x2Timer = 0;
    p.nextHitPowerMul = 1; p.nextAngleMul = 1; p.nextSpeedMul = 1; p.nextSlowReturn = false;
    if (role.onRoundStart) role.onRoundStart(p);
  }
  isChargeGated() { return !!CHARGE_GATE_INFO[this.role.id]; }
  isOncePerRound() { return this.role.cooldown >= 900; }
  hasActiveAbility() { return this.role.cooldown > 0 || this.isChargeGated(); }
  canActivate() {
    if (this.isChargeGated()) {
      const info = CHARGE_GATE_INFO[this.role.id];
      return (this.paddle[info.key] || 0) >= info.target;
    }
    if (this.isOncePerRound()) return this.cooldownRemaining <= 0;
    return this.hasActiveAbility() && this.cooldownRemaining <= 0;
  }
  activate(game) {
    if (!this.canActivate()) return false;
    const p = this.paddle;
    this.role.activate(p, game, p.side);
    if (this.isOncePerRound()) this.cooldownRemaining = 1;
    else if (!this.isChargeGated()) this.cooldownRemaining = this.role.cooldown;
    p.flareUntilPrimary = performance.now() / 1000 + 0.4;
    if (typeof startAbilityAnim === 'function') startAbilityAnim(p, this.role, 'primary');

    const glowX = p.side === 'left' ? p.x + p.width : p.x;
    game.fx.ring(glowX, p.centerY(), ROLE_RARITY[this.role.rarity].color);
    game.fx.text(p.side === 'left' ? p.x + 70 : p.x - 70, p.centerY() - 40, this.role.name.toUpperCase() + '!', ROLE_RARITY[this.role.rarity].color, 16);
    game.sfx.tone(500, 0.1, 'sine', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 900 });
    return true;
  }
  hasBonusAbility() { return !!this.role.bonusActivate; }
  canActivateBonus() { return this.hasBonusAbility() && this.bonusCooldownRemaining <= 0; }
  activateBonus(game) {
    if (!this.canActivateBonus()) return false;
    const p = this.paddle;
    this.role.bonusActivate(p, game, p.side);
    this.bonusCooldownRemaining = this.role.bonusCooldown || 10;
    p.flareUntilBonus = performance.now() / 1000 + 0.4;
    if (typeof startAbilityAnim === 'function') startAbilityAnim(p, this.role, 'bonus');

    const glowX = p.side === 'left' ? p.x + p.width : p.x;
    const rarity = ROLE_RARITY[this.role.rarity];
    game.fx.ring(glowX, p.centerY(), rarity.color);
    game.fx.text(p.side === 'left' ? p.x + 70 : p.x - 70, p.centerY() - 56, (this.role.bonusName || 'BONUS') + '!', rarity.color, 14);
    game.sfx.tone(650, 0.12, 'triangle', { gain: 0.2, slideTo: 1100 });
    return true;
  }
  bonusCooldownRatio() {
    if (!this.hasBonusAbility()) return 0;
    const cd = this.role.bonusCooldown || 10;
    return clamp(1 - this.bonusCooldownRemaining / cd, 0, 1);
  }
  update(dt) {
    if (this.cooldownRemaining > 0) this.cooldownRemaining -= dt;
    if (this.bonusCooldownRemaining > 0) this.bonusCooldownRemaining -= dt;
    const p = this.paddle;
    if (p.dashTimer > 0) { p.dashTimer -= dt; if (p.dashTimer <= 0) p.dashMul = 1; }
    if (p.wallTimer > 0) p.wallTimer -= dt;
    if (p.titanTimer > 0) p.titanTimer -= dt;
    if (p.shadowTimer > 0) p.shadowTimer -= dt;
    if (p.phantomTimer > 0) p.phantomTimer -= dt;
    if (p.overlordTimer > 0) p.overlordTimer -= dt;
    if (p.demonModeTimer > 0) p.demonModeTimer -= dt;
    if (p.godspeedTimer > 0) p.godspeedTimer -= dt;
    if (p.iceSlowTimer > 0) p.iceSlowTimer -= dt;
    if (typeof updateAbilityAnim === 'function') updateAbilityAnim(p, dt);
    if (p.scoutTimer > 0) p.scoutTimer -= dt;
    if (p.focusTimer > 0) p.focusTimer -= dt;
    if (p.braceTimer > 0) p.braceTimer -= dt;
    if (p.sizeBoostTimer > 0) p.sizeBoostTimer -= dt;
    if (p.echoTimer > 0) p.echoTimer -= dt;
    if (p.magnetTimer > 0) p.magnetTimer -= dt;
    if (p.infernoActive > 0) p.infernoActive -= dt;
    if (p.x2Timer > 0) p.x2Timer -= dt;
  }
  cooldownRatio() {
    if (this.isChargeGated()) {
      const info = CHARGE_GATE_INFO[this.role.id];
      return clamp((this.paddle[info.key] || 0) / info.target, 0, 1);
    }
    if (this.isOncePerRound()) return this.cooldownRemaining > 0 ? 0 : 1;
    if (this.role.cooldown <= 0) return 0;
    return clamp(1 - this.cooldownRemaining / this.role.cooldown, 0, 1);
  }
  applyOnHit(game, ball) { if (this.role.onHit) this.role.onHit(this.paddle, game, ball); }
  applyOnBlock() { if (this.role.onBlock) this.role.onBlock(this.paddle); }
  applyOnScore() { if (this.role.onScore) this.role.onScore(this.paddle); }
  applyOnConcede() { if (this.role.onConcede) this.role.onConcede(this.paddle); }
}

function getPassiveMul(paddle, key, fallback = 1) {
  if (!paddle.roleController) return fallback;
  const v = paddle.roleController.role.passive[key];
  return v !== undefined ? v : fallback;
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: ROLE SPINNER
   A slot-machine-style spinner that auto-spins through paddle
   roles and settles on a result for each player. Purely visual
   state machine driven from the game's countdown/reset flow.
   ============================================================ */

class RoleSpinner {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.active = false;
    this.timer = 0;
    this.totalDuration = 2.2;
    this.results = { left: null, right: null };
    this.displayIndexLeft = 0;
    this.displayIndexRight = 0;
    this.tickTimer = 0;
    this.settledLeft = false;
    this.settledRight = false;
    this.pool = PADDLE_ROLES;
    this.onComplete = null;
    this.soloMode = false;
    this.revealFlash = 0;
    this.revealParticlesSpawned = false;
    this.readyTimer = 0;
    this.readyDuration = 0;
  }
  start(onComplete, opts = {}) {
    this.active = true;
    this.timer = 0;
    this.settledLeft = false;
    this.settledRight = false;
    this.soloMode = !!opts.solo;
    this.results.left = pickWeightedRole(this.pool);
    this.results.right = this.soloMode ? null : pickWeightedRole(this.pool);
    this.onComplete = onComplete;
    this.tickTimer = 0;
    this.revealFlash = 0;
    this.revealParticlesSpawned = false;
    this.readyTimer = 0;
    this.readyDuration = 0;
    this.game.sfx.tone(300, 0.05, 'square', { gain: 0.1 });
  }
  update(dt) {
    if (!this.active) return;
    if (this.readyTimer > 0) { this.readyTimer -= dt; } // reading-time countdown after settling
    this.timer += dt;
    this.tickTimer -= dt;
    if (this.revealFlash > 0) this.revealFlash = Math.max(0, this.revealFlash - dt * 1.8);

    // spin speed decelerates over time (ease-out), producing a slot-machine feel
    const progress = clamp(this.timer / this.totalDuration, 0, 1);
    const spinSpeed = lerp(0.04, 0.22, 1 - Math.pow(progress, 2)); // seconds per tick, grows slower near the end

    if (this.tickTimer <= 0 && progress < 0.92) {
      this.displayIndexLeft = (this.displayIndexLeft + 1) % this.pool.length;
      this.displayIndexRight = (this.displayIndexRight + 1) % this.pool.length;
      this.tickTimer = spinSpeed;
      // ticking pitch rises as it approaches landing, and speeds up near the end
      this.game.sfx.tone(400 + progress * 500, 0.03, 'square', { gain: 0.05 + progress * 0.06 });
    }

    if (progress >= 1 && !this.settledLeft) {
      this.settledLeft = true;
      this.settledRight = true;
      this.onSettle();
    }
  }
  onSettle() {
    const bestRarity = this.soloMode
      ? this.results.left.rarity
      : this.higherRarity(this.results.left.rarity, this.results.right.rarity);
    // Rarer results get a bigger flash, more particles, and a much longer
    // dramatic pause before control hands back — long enough to actually
    // read the role name, rarity, and ability text, not just glimpse it.
    const rarityRank = { common: 0, rare: 1, epic: 2, legendary: 3, mythic: 4 };
    const rank = rarityRank[bestRarity] !== undefined ? rarityRank[bestRarity] : 0;
    this.revealFlash = 0.3 + rank * 0.15;
    // Base reading time is generous on its own; rarer results linger longer.
    const pauseMs = 2400 + rank * 500;

    this.game.sfx.powerup();
    if (rank >= 2) {
      // extra chime layer for epic/legendary/mythic reveals
      setTimeout(() => this.game.sfx.tone(900, 0.2, 'sine', { gain: 0.22, slideTo: 1400 }), 120);
    }
    const g = this.game;
    const w = g.width, h = g.height;
    const color = ROLE_RARITY[bestRarity].color;
    g.fx.burst(w / 2, h / 2, color, 14 + rank * 10, { maxSpeed: 260 + rank * 60, maxLife: 0.9 });
    this.readyTimer = pauseMs / 1000;
    this.readyDuration = pauseMs / 1000;
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.active = false;
      if (this.onComplete) this.onComplete(this.results.left, this.results.right);
    }, pauseMs);
  }
  higherRarity(a, b) {
    const order = ['common', 'rare', 'epic', 'legendary', 'mythic'];
    return order.indexOf(a) >= order.indexOf(b) ? a : b;
  }
  currentDisplay(side) {
    if ((side === 'left' && this.settledLeft) || (side === 'right' && this.settledRight)) {
      return this.results[side];
    }
    const idx = side === 'left' ? this.displayIndexLeft : this.displayIndexRight;
    return this.pool[idx];
  }
  draw(ctx, w, h) {
    if (!this.active) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

    if (this.revealFlash > 0 && this.settledLeft) {
      const bestRarity = this.soloMode ? this.results.left.rarity : this.higherRarity(this.results.left.rarity, this.results.right.rarity);
      const flashColor = ROLE_RARITY[bestRarity].color;
      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(w / 2, h / 2, 0, w / 2, h / 2, w * 0.6);
      grad.addColorStop(0, hexToRgba(flashColor, this.revealFlash * 0.5));
      grad.addColorStop(1, hexToRgba(flashColor, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    }

    const cardW = 220, maxCardH = 224;
    const positions = this.soloMode
      ? [{ side: 'left', x: w / 2 - cardW / 2, label: 'YOUR ROLE' }]
      : [
          { side: 'left', x: w / 2 - cardW - 20, label: 'PLAYER 1' },
          { side: 'right', x: w / 2 + 20, label: 'PLAYER 2 / CPU' },
        ];
    const y = h / 2 - maxCardH / 2 - 14;

    for (const pos of positions) {
      const role = this.currentDisplay(pos.side);
      if (!role) continue;
      const rarity = ROLE_RARITY[role.rarity];
      const settled = pos.side === 'left' ? this.settledLeft : this.settledRight;
      const pulse = settled ? 1 + Math.sin(performance.now() / 150) * 0.04 : 1;
      const bonusAbility = settled && ROLE_HAS_BONUS_ABILITY(role);
      const cardH = bonusAbility ? 224 : 150;

      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(pos.x + cardW / 2, y + maxCardH / 2);
      ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);
      ctx.translate(-cardW / 2, -cardH / 2);

      ctx.shadowColor = rarity.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = settled ? 30 : 14;
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,8,20,0.92)';
      ctx.strokeStyle = rarity.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = settled ? 3 : 1.5;
      roundRect(ctx, 0, 0, cardW, cardH, 12);
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();

      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
      ctx.font = '700 11px Orbitron, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(pos.label, cardW / 2, 20);

      ctx.font = '34px sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = rarity.color;
      ctx.fillText(role.icon, cardW / 2, 60);

      ctx.font = '700 16px Orbitron, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.fillText(role.name, cardW / 2, 86);

      ctx.font = '700 10px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = rarity.color;
      ctx.fillText(rarity.label, cardW / 2, 103);

      if (settled) {
        ctx.font = '500 10px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)';
        ctx.textAlign = 'left';
        ctx.fillText('X / ← ability:', 14, 124);
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
        wrapText(ctx, role.desc, cardW / 2, 139, cardW - 24, 13);

        if (bonusAbility) {
          ctx.textAlign = 'left';
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)';
          ctx.font = '500 10px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
          ctx.fillText('C / → bonus:', 14, 178);
          ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          ctx.fillStyle = rarity.color;
          wrapText(ctx, role.bonusDesc || 'A second unique ability.', cardW / 2, 193, cardW - 24, 13);
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '700 14px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
    ctx.fillText(this.settledLeft ? 'ROLES ASSIGNED' : 'ROLLING...', w / 2, y - 20);

    // "starting in..." reading-time progress bar, only shown once settled
    if (this.settledLeft && this.readyDuration > 0) {
      const barW = 260, barH = 6;
      const bx = w / 2 - barW / 2, by = y + maxCardH + 26;
      const ratio = clamp(1 - this.readyTimer / this.readyDuration, 0, 1);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
      roundRect(ctx, bx, by, barW, barH, 3);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)';
      roundRect(ctx, bx, by, barW * ratio, barH, 3);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.font = '600 10px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.45)';
      ctx.fillText('starting soon...', w / 2, by + 22);
    }

    ctx.restore();
  }
}

function ROLE_HAS_BONUS_ABILITY(role) {
  return !!(role && role.bonusActivate);
}

function wrapText(ctx, text, x, y, maxWidth, lineHeight) {
  const words = text.split(' ');
  let line = '';
  let curY = y;
  let lines = 0;
  for (const word of words) {
    const test = line + word + ' ';
    if (ctx.measureText(test).width > maxWidth && line !== '') {
      ctx.fillText(line.trim(), x, curY);
      line = word + ' ';
      curY += lineHeight;
      lines++;
      if (lines >= 2) return;
    } else {
      line = test;
    }
  }
  if (lines < 2) ctx.fillText(line.trim(), x, curY);
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: ROLE VISUALS
   Every one of the 67 roles gets its own hand-designed paddle
   appearance — body shape, surface treatment, idle motion, and
   an activation flare for both the primary and bonus ability.
   Rarity still escalates visual complexity (common = clean and
   simple, mythic = layered and spectacular) but every single
   role has a distinct silhouette and motif, not a shared border
   pattern with a recolor.
   ============================================================ */

function rv_body(ctx, r, fillColor, glowColor, glow) {
  ctx.shadowColor = glowColor;
  ctx.shadowBlur = glow;
  ctx.fillStyle = fillColor;
  roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5);
  ctx.fill();
}
function rv_outline(ctx, r, color, width, pad, radius, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha !== undefined ? alpha : 1;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = width;
  roundRect(ctx, r.x - pad, r.y - pad, r.w + pad * 2, r.h + pad * 2, radius);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_segments(ctx, r, count, gap, color, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  const segH = (r.h - gap * (count - 1)) / count;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y + i * (segH + gap), r.w, segH, 2); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_scales(ctx, r, color, t, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  const rows = 6;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
    const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * (r.h / rows);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(r.x + r.w / 2, y + 4, r.w / 2 + 2, Math.PI, 0);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_cracks(ctx, r, color, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const a = (i / 4) * TAU + 0.4;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * r.w * 0.7, cy + Math.sin(a) * r.h * 0.4);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_circuits(ctx, r, color, t, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  const rows = 4;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
    const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * (r.h / rows);
    const pulse = (t * 40 + i * 15) % (r.w + 10) - 5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x, y); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w, y); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(r.x + clamp(pulse, 0, r.w), y, 1.6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_orbitDots(ctx, cx, cy, radius, count, color, t, speed, alpha, dotSize) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const a = t * speed + (i / count) * TAU;
    const px = cx + Math.cos(a) * radius, py = cy + Math.sin(a) * radius * 0.5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, dotSize || 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_flameLicks(ctx, r, color, dir, t, alpha, count) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  const n = count || 4;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * (r.h / n);
    const flick = Math.sin(t * 7 + i * 1.3) * 5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(r.x + (dir < 0 ? 0 : r.w), y);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(r.x + (dir < 0 ? -12 - flick : r.w + 12 + flick), y - 5, r.x + (dir < 0 ? -18 : r.w + 18), y + 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_frostCrystals(ctx, r, color, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * (r.h / 5);
    const x = r.x + r.w / 2;
    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
      const a = (k / 6) * TAU;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + Math.cos(a) * 5, y + Math.sin(a) * 5); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_wings(ctx, r, color, dir, t, alpha, glow) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.shadowColor = color;
  ctx.shadowBlur = glow || 0;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2;
  const flap = Math.sin(t * 3) * 6;
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx, r.y + r.h * 0.15);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx + s * (22 + flap), r.y + r.h * 0.35, cx + s * (10 + flap * 0.5), r.y + r.h * 0.6);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx, r.y + r.h * 0.5);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx + s * (16 + flap * 0.7), r.y + r.h * 0.68, cx + s * 6, r.y + r.h * 0.85);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_horns(ctx, r, color, alpha) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(r.x - 3, r.y - 2); ctx.lineTo(r.x - 12, r.y - 16); ctx.lineTo(r.x - 1, r.y - 6); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(r.x + r.w + 3, r.y - 2); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w + 12, r.y - 16); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w + 1, r.y - 6); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_starfield(ctx, r, color, t, alpha, count) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  const n = count || 6;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const a = t * 0.4 + (i / n) * TAU;
    const rad = r.w / 2 + 14 + (i % 3) * 8;
    const px = cx + Math.cos(a) * rad, py = cy + Math.sin(a) * rad * 0.6;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, 1.3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
function rv_speedLines(ctx, r, color, dir, t, alpha, count) {
  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2;
  const n = count || 4;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const off = i * 11 + ((t * 260) % 11);
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * (1 - i / n);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx + dir * off, r.y); ctx.lineTo(cx + dir * (off + 16), r.y + r.h);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

const ROLE_VISUALS = {};
function defineVisual(id, accent, idle) {
  ROLE_VISUALS[id] = { accent, idle };
}

/* ---------------- COMMON ---------------- */
defineVisual('rookie', '#bcd6ff', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 10 + (p.sizeBoostTimer > 0 ? 14 : 0));
  rv_outline(ctx, r, '#bcd6ff', 1.5, p.sizeBoostTimer > 0 ? 5 : 2, 6, p.sizeBoostTimer > 0 ? 0.9 : 0.35);
});
defineVisual('speedster', '#7ee8ff', (ctx, r, color, t) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 12);
  rv_speedLines(ctx, r, '#7ee8ff', -1, t, 0.35, 2);
  rv_speedLines(ctx, r, '#7ee8ff', 1, t, 0.35, 2);
});
defineVisual('defender', '#9fd6c0', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.braceTimer > 0 ? 20 : 10);
  rv_outline(ctx, r, '#9fd6c0', p.braceTimer > 0 ? 3 : 1.5, 3, 6, p.braceTimer > 0 ? 0.9 : 0.4);
});
defineVisual('striker', '#ff9f6b', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.nextHitPowerMul > 1 ? 20 : 10);
  ctx.globalAlpha = p.nextHitPowerMul > 1 ? 0.9 : 0.3;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff9f6b';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x + r.w / 2, r.y - 6); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w / 2 - 5, r.y + 4); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w / 2 + 5, r.y + 4); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});
defineVisual('guardian', '#8fd8ff', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.shieldHits > 0 ? 18 : 9);
  if (p.shieldHits > 0) rv_outline(ctx, r, '#8fd8ff', 2, 5, 8, 0.6 + Math.sin(t * 3) * 0.2);
});
defineVisual('bouncer', '#c9ff8f', (ctx, r, color, t) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 10);
  rv_outline(ctx, r, '#c9ff8f', 1.5, 2, 3, 0.4);
});
defineVisual('charger', '#ffe08f', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.nextHitPowerMul > 1 ? 18 : 9);
  rv_circuits(ctx, r, '#ffe08f', t, 0.35);
});
defineVisual('acrobat', '#ffd1f0', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.dashTimer > 0 ? 20 : 9);
  rv_speedLines(ctx, r, '#ffd1f0', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, t, p.dashTimer > 0 ? 0.7 : 0, 2);
});
defineVisual('wall_c', '#c9c9c9', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.giantTimer > 0 ? 18 : 8);
  rv_segments(ctx, r, 4, 3, '#c9c9c9', 0.3);
});
defineVisual('sharpshooter', '#ff8f8f', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.sniperCharge ? 22 : 9);
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  ctx.globalAlpha = p.sniperCharge ? 0.9 : 0.3;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff8f8f'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, 9, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx - 13, cy); ctx.lineTo(cx + 13, cy); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - 13); ctx.lineTo(cx, cy + 13); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});
defineVisual('runner', '#8fffb0', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.dashTimer > 0 ? 18 : 9);
  rv_speedLines(ctx, r, '#8fffb0', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, t, p.dashTimer > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.15, 2);
});
defineVisual('magnet_c', '#ffcf5c', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.magnetTimer > 0 ? 18 : 8);
  const cx = p.side === 'left' ? r.x + r.w : r.x, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  rv_orbitDots(ctx, cx, cy, 14, 3, '#ffcf5c', t, 2, p.magnetTimer > 0 ? 0.8 : 0.3, 2);
});
defineVisual('flicker', '#e0b3ff', (ctx, r, color, t) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 10);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e0b3ff';
  ctx.fillRect(r.x, r.y + r.h * 0.3, r.w, 2);
  ctx.fillRect(r.x, r.y + r.h * 0.7, r.w, 2);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});
defineVisual('heavy', '#b0b0b0', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.nextHitPowerMul > 1 ? 20 : 8);
  rv_segments(ctx, r, 3, 4, '#707070', 0.5);
});
defineVisual('lightfoot', '#c8ffe8', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.dashTimer > 0 ? 16 : 8);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#c8ffe8'; ctx.setLineDash([2, 3]); ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  roundRect(ctx, r.x - 2, r.y - 2, r.w + 4, r.h + 4, 6); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});
defineVisual('guard', '#a0c4ff', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.guardCharge > 0 ? 18 : 8);
  if (p.guardCharge > 0) rv_outline(ctx, r, '#a0c4ff', 2.5, 3, 6, 0.7);
});
defineVisual('spark', '#fff08f', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, p.sparkNextHit ? 18 : 9);
  ctx.globalAlpha = p.sparkNextHit ? 0.8 : 0.3;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff08f'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * r.h / 3;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x + r.w, y); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w + 5 + Math.sin(t * 10 + i) * 2, y - 3); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});
defineVisual('flame_c', '#ff9040', (ctx, r, color, t, p) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 12);
  rv_flameLicks(ctx, r, '#ff9040', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, t, 0.55, 3);
});
defineVisual('frost_c', '#a0e8ff', (ctx, r, color, t) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 12);
  rv_frostCrystals(ctx, r, '#a0e8ff', 0.4);
});
defineVisual('wind', '#d0fff0', (ctx, r, color, t) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 10);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35; ctx.strokeStyle = '#d0fff0'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * r.h / 3 + Math.sin(t * 2 + i) * 3;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x - 8, y); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(r.x - 2, y - 4, r.x + 4, y); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});
defineVisual('pulse_c', '#b0b0ff', (ctx, r, color, t) => {
  rv_body(ctx, r, color, color, 10);
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  const rad = 10 + ((t * 30) % 20);
  ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, 0.5 - rad / 40);
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#b0b0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
});

/* ============================================================
   ROLE_ANIM — full activation -> active -> hit -> ending sequences.
   Populated tier by tier (Mythic + Legendary first, since those
   carry the most spec detail); every entry below overrides the
   generic default phase renderer with bespoke, rarity-appropriate
   VFX and — where the role affects the ball — a real hook that
   colors the ball's trail/aura via ball.setRoleAura(color, ms).
   ============================================================ */
const ROLE_ANIM = {}; // id -> { phases: {activate:{dur,draw}, active:{...}, hit:{...}, ending:{...}} }

/* ---------- MYTHIC ---------- */
ROLE_ANIM.demon = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.5, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      // paddle shakes as dark energy gathers
      const shake = (1 - lt) * 3;
      ctx.translate(rand(-shake, shake), rand(-shake, shake));
      ctx.globalAlpha = lt;
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff2b4d'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff2b4d';
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const a = lt * TAU * 2 + (i / 6) * TAU;
        const rad = lerp(40, 8, lt);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(a) * rad, cy + Math.sin(a) * rad * 0.6, 2.5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      rv_horns(ctx, r, '#ff2b4d', lt);
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 2.0, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff2b4d'; ctx.shadowBlur = 40;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff2b4d';
      roundRect(ctx, r.x - 5, r.y - 5, r.w + 10, r.h + 10, 8); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25; ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9;
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        const a = performance.now() / 250 + (i / 5) * TAU;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(a) * 26, cy + Math.sin(a) * 26, 2.4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      rv_horns(ctx, r, '#ff2b4d', 1);
      rv_cracks(ctx, r, '#ff2b4d', 0.6);
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    hit: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      const rad = lerp(r.w * 0.5, r.w * 4, lt);
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.95;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff2b4d'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ff2b4d'; ctx.shadowBlur = 40;
      ctx.lineWidth = 4;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.6;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r.w * 0.4 * (1 - lt), 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.5, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.7;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff2b4d'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ff2b4d'; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
      const rad = lerp(50, 5, lt);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
  },
};

/* ---------- LEGENDARY ---------- */
ROLE_ANIM.thunder = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      // storm clouds darken the top of the arena briefly
      if (game) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.35;
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 90);
        grad.addColorStop(0, '#1a1a2e'); grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(26,26,46,0)');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, game.width, 90);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = lt;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe650'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe650'; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const y = r.y + (i + 0.5) * r.h / 3;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x - 8, y); ctx.lineTo(r.x - 3, y - 3); ctx.lineTo(r.x - 6, y); ctx.lineTo(r.x - 1, y + 4); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.25, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      // the big bolt itself: a jagged line from the top of the arena to the ball
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (!ball) return;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - lt * 0.3;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe650'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      let x = ball.x, y = 0;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      while (y < ball.y) {
        x += rand(-18, 18); y += rand(20, 40);
        ctx.lineTo(clamp(x, 10, (game.width || 960) - 10), Math.min(y, ball.y));
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.8;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, 30 * (1 - lt) + 10, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
      if (lt > 0.4 && !p._thunderApplied) {
        p._thunderApplied = true;
        ball.setRoleAura('#ffe650', 2600);
        game.fx.shake(game.settings.screenShake ? 10 : 0, 4);
      }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      p._thunderApplied = false;
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.5;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe650'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe650'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, lerp(10, 40, lt), 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
  },
};
ROLE_ANIM.dragon = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      rv_scales(ctx, r, '#ff8800', performance.now() / 1000, 0.5);
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt; ctx.shadowColor = '#ff8800'; ctx.shadowBlur = 26;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff8800';
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx, r.y - 10, 10 * lt, 6 * lt, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.5, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      rv_flameLicks(ctx, r, '#ffaa00', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, performance.now() / 1000, 0.9, 5);
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (lt > 0.3 && ball && !p._dragonApplied) {
        p._dragonApplied = true;
        ball.setRoleAura('#ff8800', 3000);
      }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      p._dragonApplied = false;
      rv_flameLicks(ctx, r, '#ffaa00', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, performance.now() / 1000, (1 - lt) * 0.6, 3);
    } },
  },
};
ROLE_ANIM.reaper = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.8; ctx.fillStyle = '#a020c0';
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        const y = r.y + rand(0, r.h);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(r.x + r.w / 2 + rand(-14, 14), y, 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y - 10;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.8;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#a020c0'; ctx.shadowColor = '#a020c0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      const sweep = lerp(-0.8, 0.8, lt);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx + Math.cos(sweep) * 26, cy - 16, cx + Math.cos(sweep) * 36, cy - 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    hit: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball && !p._reaperApplied) { p._reaperApplied = true; ball.setRoleAura('#a020c0', 1800); }
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.85;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#a020c0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 26; ctx.shadowColor = '#a020c0'; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, lerp(r.w * 0.4, r.w * 2.2, lt), 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) { p._reaperApplied = false; } },
  },
};

    } },

      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();

        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.45;
        ctx.strokeStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? '#8060ff' : '#60c0ff';
        const rad = r.w / 2 + 10 + i * 10;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, performance.now() / 400 + i, performance.now() / 400 + i + Math.PI); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball && !p._galaxyApplied) { p._galaxyApplied = true; ball.setRoleAura('#8060ff', 4000); }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) { p._galaxyApplied = false; } },
  },
};

    } },
    active: { dur: 0.5, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.6 + Math.sin(lt * TAU * 2) * 0.2;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff8dd'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff8dd'; ctx.shadowBlur = 26; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r.w / 2 + 20, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
      rv_wings(ctx, r, '#fff8dd', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, performance.now() / 1000, 0.9, 16);
    } },
    hit: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff8dd'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff8dd'; ctx.shadowBlur = 34; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, lerp(r.w * 0.5, r.w * 3, lt), 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      // scattering feather-like particles
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff8dd';
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const a = (i / 6) * TAU;
        const rad = lerp(0, r.w * 2, lt);
        ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.7;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(a) * rad, cy + Math.sin(a) * rad * 0.6, 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      rv_wings(ctx, r, '#fff8dd', p.side === 'left' ? -1 : 1, performance.now() / 1000, (1 - lt) * 0.5, 8);
    } },
  },
};

ROLE_ANIM.void = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.6;
      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, lerp(4, r.w * 1.6, lt));
      grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(160,32,224,0.6)'); grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(160,32,224,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r.w * 1.6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball && !p._voidApplied) { p._voidApplied = true; ball.setRoleAura('#a020e0', 1400); }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) { p._voidApplied = false; } },
  },
};

    activate: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.6; ctx.strokeStyle = '#7040ff'; ctx.setLineDash([4, 3]); ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x - lerp(0, 8, lt), r.y - lerp(0, 8, lt), r.w + lerp(0, 16, lt), r.h + lerp(0, 16, lt), 8);
      ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball && !p._dimensionApplied) {
        p._dimensionApplied = true;
        ball.setRoleAura('#7040ff', 900);
        game.fx.ring(ball.x, ball.y, '#7040ff');
      }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) { p._dimensionApplied = false; } },
  },
};

  phases: {

      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.6; ctx.fillStyle = '#8060a0';
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(r.x + r.w / 2 + rand(-8, 8), r.y + rand(0, r.h), 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35; ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
      roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5); ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#8060a0'; ctx.shadowColor = '#8060a0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x - 2, r.y - 2, r.w + 4, r.h + 4, 6); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.4; ctx.fillStyle = '#8060a0';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + r.h / 2, r.w * 1.5 * lt, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
  },
};
ROLE_ANIM.ice = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.2, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball) {
        ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.6; ctx.strokeStyle = '#a8e8ff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#a8e8ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x + (p.side === 'left' ? r.w : 0), r.y + r.h / 2); ctx.lineTo(ball.x, ball.y); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      }
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.9, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (!ball) return;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.75;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#a8e8ff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#a8e8ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      for (let layer = 0; layer < 3; layer++) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius + 3 + layer * 3, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      }
      // falling snowflakes around the frozen ball
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        const sy = ball.y - 14 + ((performance.now() / 40 + i * 20) % 28);
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x + Math.sin(i * 2) * 12, sy, 1.3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      // faint cracks that grow as the freeze nears its end
      if (lt > 0.6) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = (lt - 0.6) * 2;
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ball.x - 6, ball.y - 6); ctx.lineTo(ball.x + 5, ball.y + 4); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (!ball) return;
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.8; ctx.fillStyle = '#a8e8ff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#a8e8ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        const a = (i / 8) * TAU;
        const rad = lt * 20;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x + Math.cos(a) * rad, ball.y + Math.sin(a) * rad, 1.6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
  },
};

      ctx.restore();

        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ball.x - 14 - i * 6, ball.y); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ball.x - 8 - i * 6, ball.y - 4, ball.x - 2 - i * 6, ball.y); ctx.stroke();
        }
        ctx.restore();
      }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.2 },
  },

  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.2, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.5; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe8b0'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        const a = (i / 4) * TAU + lt * TAU;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(a) * 8, cy + Math.sin(a) * 8, 1, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.35, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.4; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe8b0'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, 8, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.2 },
  },

ROLE_ANIM.scout = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.2, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball) {
        ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.4; ctx.strokeStyle = '#b0ffe8'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + r.h / 2); ctx.lineTo(ball.x, ball.y); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      }
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball) {
        ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35; ctx.strokeStyle = '#b0ffe8'; ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]); ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        let px = ball.x, py = ball.y, vx = ball.vx, vy = ball.vy;
        ctx.moveTo(px, py);
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
          px += vx * 0.02; py += vy * 0.02;
          if (py < 10 || py > (game.height - 10)) vy *= -1;
          ctx.lineTo(px, py);
        }
        ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.2 },
  },
};
