    hit: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - lt) * 0.9; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff0a0'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff0a0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22;
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const a = (i / 6) * TAU;
        const rad = lerp(0, r.w * 1.8, lt);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(a) * rad, cy + Math.sin(a) * rad, 1.6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.2 },
  },
};
ROLE_ANIM.trickster = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.2, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.4; ctx.fillStyle = '#e0a0ff';
      roundRect(ctx, r.x + lt * 4, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.3, draw(ctx, r, p, lt, rarity, game) {
      const ball = game && game.balls[0];
      if (ball && !p._tricksterApplied) { p._tricksterApplied = true; ball.colorFlickerUntil = performance.now() + 900; }
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.2, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) { p._tricksterApplied = false; } },
  },
};
ROLE_ANIM.balanced = {
  phases: {
    activate: { dur: 0.25, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = lt * 0.5; ctx.strokeStyle = '#d0d0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      roundRect(ctx, r.x - lerp(0, 5, lt), r.y - lerp(0, 5, lt), r.w + lerp(0, 10, lt), r.h + lerp(0, 10, lt), 7); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } },
    active: { dur: 0.4, draw(ctx, r, p, lt) {
      rv_orbitDots(ctx, r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + r.h / 2, r.w / 2 + 10, 3, '#d0d0ff', performance.now() / 1000, 1.2, 0.4, 1.5);
    } },
    ending: { dur: 0.25 },
  },
};

function drawRoleFlare(ctx, r, role, kind) {
  const color = kind === 'bonus' ? ((ROLE_RARITY[role.rarity] || {}).color || '#ffffff') : ((ROLE_VISUALS[role.id] || {}).accent || '#ffffff');
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.8;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.shadowColor = color;
  ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx, cy, r.w * 1.6, 0, TAU);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

function startAbilityAnim(paddle, role, kind) {
  const now = performance.now() / 1000;
  const anim = ROLE_ANIM[role.id];
  const order = ['activate', 'active', 'ending'];
  paddle.abilityAnim = {
    role, kind, order, phaseIndex: 0,
    phaseStart: now,
    anim: anim || null,
    hitTriggered: false,
  };
}
function triggerAbilityHitPhase(paddle) {
  // called from ball-collision code when a paddle with an in-flight ability
  // anim actually strikes the ball, so the 'hit' phase can insert itself
  const st = paddle.abilityAnim;
  if (!st || st.hitTriggered) return;
  st.hitTriggered = true;
  const now = performance.now() / 1000;
  // splice a 'hit' phase in right after whatever phase is currently playing
  if (st.order.indexOf('hit') === -1) {
    st.order.splice(st.phaseIndex + 1, 0, 'hit');
  }
}
function currentAnimPhaseDuration(st, phaseName) {
  const custom = st.anim && st.anim.phases && st.anim.phases[phaseName];
  if (custom && custom.dur) return custom.dur;
  const defaults = { activate: 0.25, active: 0.6, hit: 0.28, ending: 0.35 };
  return defaults[phaseName] || 0.3;
}
function updateAbilityAnim(paddle, dt) {
  const st = paddle.abilityAnim;
  if (!st) return;
  const now = performance.now() / 1000;
  const phaseName = st.order[st.phaseIndex];
  const dur = currentAnimPhaseDuration(st, phaseName);
  if (now - st.phaseStart >= dur) {
    st.phaseIndex++;
    st.phaseStart = now;
    if (st.phaseIndex >= st.order.length) {
      paddle.abilityAnim = null;
    }
  }
}
function drawAbilityAnim(ctx, game, paddle, r) {
  const st = paddle.abilityAnim;
  if (!st) return;
  const now = performance.now() / 1000;
  const phaseName = st.order[st.phaseIndex];
  const dur = currentAnimPhaseDuration(st, phaseName);
  const localT = clamp((now - st.phaseStart) / dur, 0, 1);
  const rarity = ROLE_RARITY[st.role.rarity];
  const custom = st.anim && st.anim.phases && st.anim.phases[phaseName];
  if (custom && custom.draw) {
    custom.draw(ctx, r, paddle, localT, rarity, game);
  } else {
    drawDefaultAnimPhase(ctx, r, paddle, phaseName, localT, rarity);
  }
}
/* Fallback phase rendering for any role without a custom ROLE_ANIM entry —
   still rarity-scaled and phase-distinct, just simpler than a bespoke one. */
function drawDefaultAnimPhase(ctx, r, paddle, phaseName, localT, rarity) {
  const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
  ctx.save();
  if (phaseName === 'activate') {
    const rad = lerp(r.w * 0.6, r.w * 1.8, localT);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - localT;
    ctx.strokeStyle = rarity.color;
    ctx.shadowColor = rarity.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  } else if (phaseName === 'active') {
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5 + Math.sin(localT * TAU * 3) * 0.2;
    ctx.strokeStyle = rarity.color; ctx.shadowColor = rarity.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    roundRect(ctx, r.x - 4, r.y - 4, r.w + 8, r.h + 8, 8); ctx.stroke();
  } else if (phaseName === 'hit') {
    const rad = lerp(r.w * 0.3, r.w * 2.4, localT);
    ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - localT) * 0.9;
    ctx.fillStyle = rarity.color; ctx.shadowColor = rarity.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 26;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  } else if (phaseName === 'ending') {
    ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - localT) * 0.6;
    ctx.strokeStyle = rarity.color; ctx.shadowColor = rarity.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    const rad = lerp(r.w * 1.2, r.w * 0.4, localT);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawRolePaddle(ctx, game, paddle, r) {
  const rc = paddle.roleController;
  const role = rc ? rc.role : null;
  const visual = role ? ROLE_VISUALS[role.id] : null;
  const t = performance.now() / 1000;

  ctx.save();
  if (visual) {
    visual.idle(ctx, r, paddle.color, t, paddle);
  } else {
    rv_body(ctx, r, paddle.color, paddle.color, 14);
  }
  if (paddle.giantTimer > 0) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    roundRect(ctx, r.x - 3, r.y - 3, r.w + 6, r.h + 6, 7);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();

  if (paddle.abilityAnim) drawAbilityAnim(ctx, game, paddle, r);
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: GAME CONTROLLER
   Ties together core, fx, powerups, ai, creative into modes:
   - classic (vs AI)
   - twoplayer (local)
   - tournament (best-of series vs escalating AI)
   - survival (single paddle vs multiplying balls)
   - creative (draw mode)
   ============================================================ */

const THEMES = {
  cyber: { name: 'Cyber Cyan/Magenta', p1: '#ff00d4', p2: '#00f0ff', ball: '#ffffff', bg1: '#05010a', bg2: '#0a0018', accent: '#00f0ff' },
  sunset: { name: 'Vapor Sunset', p1: '#ff6ec7', p2: '#ffd23f', ball: '#ffffff', bg1: '#1a0533', bg2: '#3d0f4f', accent: '#ff6ec7' },
  matrix: { name: 'Matrix Green', p1: '#00ff41', p2: '#00b32d', ball: '#e6ffe9', bg1: '#000800', bg2: '#001a05', accent: '#00ff41' },
  inferno: { name: 'Inferno', p1: '#ff3300', p2: '#ffaa00', ball: '#ffffff', bg1: '#0d0000', bg2: '#1a0500', accent: '#ff3300' },
  ice: { name: 'Ice Storm', p1: '#00d9ff', p2: '#7de2ff', ball: '#ffffff', bg1: '#000814', bg2: '#001529', accent: '#00d9ff' },
  royal: { name: 'Royal Violet', p1: '#a855f7', p2: '#f472b6', ball: '#ffffff', bg1: '#0a0014', bg2: '#170029', accent: '#a855f7' },
};

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = () => ({
  paddleSpeed: 560,
  ballSpeed: 380,
  maxBallSpeed: 980,
  winScore: 7,
  lives: 3,
  glowIntensity: 1,
  screenShake: true,
  powerupsEnabled: true,
  enabledPowerups: Object.fromEntries(POWERUP_TYPES.map(t => [t.id, true])),
  theme: 'cyber',
  aiDifficulty: 'pro',
  soundOn: true,
  musicOn: false,
  sfxVolume: 0.5,
  particlesEnabled: true,
  paddleSize: 100,
  showFps: false,
  arenaLayout: 'none',
  rolesEnabled: false,
  anyRolesEnabled: false,
  anyRolesPickId: null, // last role the player picked, reused as the default highlight next round
  fullscreenOnLaunch: false,
  skinP1: 'classic',
  skinP2: 'classic',
  colorblindMode: false,
  reducedMotion: false,
  ghostEnabled: false,
  timeAttackDuration: 60,
});

class Game {
  constructor(canvas) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    this.ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    this.width = 960;
    this.height = 540;
    this.settings = this.loadSettings();
    this.state = 'menu'; // menu | countdown | playing | paused | gameover | creative
    this.mode = 'classic'; // classic | twoplayer | tournament | survival | creative
    this.balls = [];
    this.fx = new FXManager(this);
    this.sfx = new SFX();
    this.sfx.setVolume(this.settings.soundOn ? this.settings.sfxVolume : 0);
    this.powerups = new PowerupManager(this);
    this.spinner = new RoleSpinner(this);
    this.timeDilation = null;
    this.creative = new CreativeModeController(this);
    this.obstacles = new ObstacleManager(this);
    this.achievements = new AchievementManager(this);
    this.ghost = new GhostRecorder(this);
    this.timeAttack = new TimeAttackController(this);
    this.history = new MatchHistory();
    this.boss = null;
    this.keys = {};
    this.paddles = [];
    this.ai = null;
    this.countdownVal = 3;
    this.countdownTimer = 0;
    this.winner = null;
    this.tournamentRound = 0;
    this.tournamentOpponents = ['rookie', 'amateur', 'pro', 'legend'];
    this.survivalTime = 0;
    this.survivalBallTimer = 0;
    this.stats = this.loadStats();
    this.lastTime = performance.now();
    this.fpsHistory = [];
    this.paused = false;
    this.freeForAllMode = false;
    this.rallyCount = 0;
    this.bestRally = this.stats.bestRally || 0;
    this.screenFlashCooldown = 0;
    this.touchP1Target = null;
    this.touchP2Target = null;
  }

  loadSettings() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('neonpong_settings');
      if (raw) return Object.assign(DEFAULT_SETTINGS(), JSON.parse(raw));
    } catch (e) {}
    return DEFAULT_SETTINGS();
  }
  saveSettings() {
    try { localStorage.setItem('neonpong_settings', JSON.stringify(this.settings)); } catch (e) {}
  }
  loadStats() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('neonpong_stats');
      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
    return { wins: 0, losses: 0, gamesPlayed: 0, bestRally: 0, tournamentWins: 0, survivalBest: 0, totalPoints: 0,
      beatLegend: false, creativeRally: false, bricksCleared: false, chaosWins: 0, bossDefeated: false };
  }
  saveStats() {
    try { localStorage.setItem('neonpong_stats', JSON.stringify(this.stats)); } catch (e) {}
    this.achievements.checkAll();
  }

  theme() { return getEffectiveTheme(this); }

  getPaddleBySide(side) {
    return this.paddles.find(p => p.side === side);
  }

  /* ---------------- Mode setup ---------------- */
  setupClassic() {
    this.mode = 'classic';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: th.p2, height: h }),
    ];
    this.ai = new AIController(this, this.paddles[1], this.settings.aiDifficulty);
    this.obstacles.setLayout(this.settings.arenaLayout);
    this.resetRound();
  }
  setupTwoPlayer() {
    this.mode = 'twoplayer';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: th.p2, height: h }),
    ];
    this.ai = null;
    this.obstacles.setLayout(this.settings.arenaLayout);
    this.resetRound();
  }
  setupTournament() {
    this.mode = 'tournament';
    this.tournamentRound = 0;
    this.startTournamentRound();
  }
  startTournamentRound() {
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: th.p2, height: h }),
    ];
    const diff = this.tournamentOpponents[Math.min(this.tournamentRound, this.tournamentOpponents.length - 1)];
    this.ai = new AIController(this, this.paddles[1], diff);
    this.paddles[0].score = 0;
    this.paddles[1].score = 0;
    this.resetRound();
  }
  setupSurvival() {
    this.mode = 'survival';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    // survival: player on left, right wall is the "enemy" (no paddle, just a wall that ends game if hit)
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
    ];
    this.ai = null;
    this.survivalTime = 0;
    this.survivalBallTimer = 6;
    this.balls = [];
    this.spawnBall(1);
    this.powerups.reset();
    this.obstacles.setLayout(this.settings.arenaLayout);
    this.fx.particles = [];
    this.paddles[0].reset();
    this.beginCountdown();
  }
  setupCreative() {
    this.mode = 'creative';
    this.state = 'creative';
    this.creative.reset();
    this.balls = [];
    this.spawnBall(Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
    this.paddles = [];
    this.obstacles.setLayout('none');
  }
  setupChaos() {
    this.mode = 'chaos';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: th.p2, height: h }),
    ];
    this.ai = new AIController(this, this.paddles[1], this.settings.aiDifficulty);
    this.obstacles.setLayout(choice(['bumpers', 'portals', 'wind', 'gauntlet']));
    this.chaosShrinkTimer = 8;
    this.resetRound();
  }
  setupTimeAttack() {
    this.mode = 'timeattack';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: th.p2, height: h }),
    ];
    this.ai = new AIController(this, this.paddles[1], this.settings.aiDifficulty);
    this.obstacles.setLayout('none');
    this.timeAttack.start(this.settings.timeAttackDuration);
    this.paddles[0].score = 0;
    this.paddles[1].score = 0;
    this.resetRound();
  }
  setupBoss() {
    this.mode = 'boss';
    this.boss = new BossController(this);
    this.boss.setup();
    this.ai = this.boss.ai;
    this.resetRound();
  }
  setupPractice() {
    this.mode = 'practice';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)', height: this.height }),
    ];
    this.paddles[1].y = 0;
    this.paddles[1].isWall = true;
    this.ai = null;
    this.obstacles.setLayout('none');
    this.balls = [];
    this.spawnBall(-1);
    this.powerups.reset();
    this.state = 'playing';
  }
  setupOrbs(opponentType) {
    // opponentType: 'cpu' | 'twoplayer'
    this.mode = 'orbs';
    this.orbsOpponentType = opponentType || 'cpu';
    const th = this.theme();
    const h = 40 + (this.settings.paddleSize / 100) * 80;
    this.paddles = [
      new Paddle(this, 'left', { color: th.p1, height: h }),
      new Paddle(this, 'right', { color: th.p2, height: h }),
    ];
    this.ai = this.orbsOpponentType === 'cpu' ? new AIController(this, this.paddles[1], this.settings.aiDifficulty) : null;
    this.obstacles.setLayout('none');
    this.tempBarriers = [];
    this.resetRound();
  }

  resetRound(servingDir) {
    this.balls = [];
    this.spawnBall(servingDir || (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1));
    this.powerups.reset();
    this.fx.particles = [];
    this.rallyCount = 0;
    this.timeDilation = null;
    for (const p of this.paddles) {
      p.reset(); p.shrink = 1; p.giantTimer = 0; p.stunTimer = 0; p.inverted = false;
      p.dashTimer = 0; p.dashMul = 1; p.wallTimer = 0; p.titanTimer = 0; p.twinTimer = 0;
      p.sniperCharge = false; p.vortexWell = null;
    }
    const rolesApplicable = this.settings.rolesEnabled && this.paddles.length === 2 &&
      this.mode !== 'creative' && this.mode !== 'practice';
    if (rolesApplicable) {
      this.ensureRoleControllers();
      const isBoss = this.mode === 'boss';
      const p2IsHuman = this.mode === 'twoplayer' || (this.mode === 'orbs' && this.orbsOpponentType === 'twoplayer');
      if (isBoss) this.paddles[1].roleController = null; // boss keeps its own phase-based system, never a spun/picked role

      if (this.settings.anyRolesEnabled) {
        // Player(s) manually pick their own role; any CPU opponent still
        // gets a random spin. In a human-vs-human match both sides pick.
        this.state = 'picking';
        this.pendingPicks = { left: false, right: p2IsHuman ? false : true };
        this.ui.showRolePicker('left', (role) => {
          this.paddles[0].roleController.assign(role);
          this.settings.anyRolesPickId = role.id;
          this.saveSettings();
          this.pendingPicks.left = true;
          this.maybeFinishPicking();
        });
        if (p2IsHuman) {
          this.ui.showRolePicker('right', (role) => {
            this.paddles[1].roleController.assign(role);
            this.pendingPicks.right = true;
            this.maybeFinishPicking();
          });
        } else if (!isBoss) {
          // CPU opponent: resolve with a quick random assignment, no spinner UI needed
          this.paddles[1].roleController.assign(pickWeightedRole(PADDLE_ROLES));
        }
      } else {
        this.state = 'spinning';
        this.spinner.start((leftRole, rightRole) => {
          this.paddles[0].roleController.assign(leftRole);
          if (!isBoss) {
            this.paddles[1].roleController.assign(rightRole);
          } else {
            this.paddles[1].roleController = null;
          }
          this.beginCountdown();
        }, { solo: isBoss });
      }
    } else {
      this.beginCountdown();
    }
  }
  maybeFinishPicking() {
    if (this.pendingPicks.left && this.pendingPicks.right) {
      this.ui.hideRolePicker();
      this.state = 'playing'; // transient; beginCountdown sets the real state next
      this.beginCountdown();
    }
  }
  ensureRoleControllers() {
    for (const p of this.paddles) {
      if (!p.roleController) p.roleController = new RoleController(p);
    }
  }
  beginCountdown() {
    this.state = 'countdown';
    this.countdownVal = 3;
    this.countdownTimer = 1;
    this.sfx.countdown(false);
  }
  spawnBall(dir) {
    const th = this.theme();
    const ball = new Ball(this, { color: th.ball, dir });
    this.balls.push(ball);
    return ball;
  }
  spawnExtraBalls(n, sourceBall) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const th = this.theme();
      const b = new Ball(this, { color: th.ball });
      // offset slightly from the source so it doesn't sit exactly on a power-up orb's
      // center and immediately re-trigger a collision on the next pass
      b.x = sourceBall.x + rand(-6, 6);
      b.y = sourceBall.y + rand(-6, 6);
      const angle = rand(0, TAU);
      const speed = sourceBall.currentSpeed();
      b.vx = Math.cos(angle) * speed;
      b.vy = Math.sin(angle) * speed;
      this.balls.push(b);
    }
    this.fx.ring(sourceBall.x, sourceBall.y, '#ffe600');
  }
  updateTempBarriers(dt) {
    if (!this.tempBarriers || this.tempBarriers.length === 0) return;
    for (const b of this.tempBarriers) b.life -= dt;
    this.tempBarriers = this.tempBarriers.filter(b => b.life > 0);
  }
  resolveTempBarriers(ball) {
    if (!this.tempBarriers) return;
    for (const b of this.tempBarriers) {
      if (circleRectCollide(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius, b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h)) {
        ball.vx *= -1;
        ball.x += ball.vx > 0 ? 10 : -10;
        this.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, b.color, 8, { maxSpeed: 140 });
        this.sfx.wallHit();
      }
    }
  }
  drawTempBarriers(ctx) {
    if (!this.tempBarriers) return;
    for (const b of this.tempBarriers) {
      const alpha = Math.min(1, b.life);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5 + alpha * 0.3;
      ctx.shadowColor = b.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
      ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(b.color, 0.25);
      ctx.strokeStyle = b.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
      ctx.strokeRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  /* ---------------- Input ---------------- */
  handleInput(dt) {
    if (this.state !== 'playing') return;
    const p1 = this.paddles[0];
    const p2 = this.paddles[1];

    // Timekeeper's Time Stop: the activating side moves at full speed while
    // everything else (including the opposing paddle) is dilated.
    const dilation = this.timeDilation;
    const dt1 = dilation && dilation.exemptSide !== 'left' ? dt * dilation.factor : dt;
    const dt2 = dilation && dilation.exemptSide !== 'right' ? dt * dilation.factor : dt;

    const useAiBranch = this.mode === 'classic' || this.mode === 'tournament' || this.mode === 'survival' ||
        this.mode === 'chaos' || this.mode === 'timeattack' || this.mode === 'boss' || this.mode === 'practice' ||
        (this.mode === 'orbs' && this.orbsOpponentType === 'cpu');
    const useTwoPlayerBranch = this.mode === 'twoplayer' || (this.mode === 'orbs' && this.orbsOpponentType === 'twoplayer');

    if (useAiBranch) {
      let dir = 0;
      if (this.keys['w'] || this.keys['arrowup']) dir -= 1;
      if (this.keys['s'] || this.keys['arrowdown']) dir += 1;
      if (this.touchP1Target !== null) {
        p1.moveTo(this.touchP1Target, dt1);
      } else if (dir !== 0) {
        p1.vy = dir * p1.speed;
        p1.moveBy(dir, dt1);
      } else { p1.vy = 0; }
      if (this.settings.ghostEnabled) this.ghost.sample(p1.y);
      if (this.ai) this.ai.update(dt2);
    } else if (useTwoPlayerBranch) {
      let dir1 = 0, dir2 = 0;
      if (this.keys['w']) dir1 -= 1;
      if (this.keys['s']) dir1 += 1;
      if (this.keys['arrowup']) dir2 -= 1;
      if (this.keys['arrowdown']) dir2 += 1;
      if (this.touchP1Target !== null) p1.moveTo(this.touchP1Target, dt1);
      else { p1.vy = dir1 * p1.speed; if (dir1) p1.moveBy(dir1, dt1); else p1.vy = 0; }
      if (this.touchP2Target !== null) p2.moveTo(this.touchP2Target, dt2);
      else { p2.vy = dir2 * p2.speed; if (dir2) p2.moveBy(dir2, dt2); else p2.vy = 0; }
    }
  }

  updateRoles(dt) {
    if (!this.settings.rolesEnabled) return;
    for (const p of this.paddles) {
      if (p.roleController) p.roleController.update(dt);
    }
    const p1 = this.paddles[0];
    const p2 = this.paddles[1];
    // Edge-triggered activation so holding the key doesn't fire every frame.
    if (p1 && p1.roleController) {
      if (this.keys['x'] && !this._prevKeyX) p1.roleController.activate(this);
      if (this.keys['c'] && !this._prevKeyC) p1.roleController.activateBonus(this);
    }
    this._prevKeyX = !!this.keys['x'];
    this._prevKeyC = !!this.keys['c'];

    const p2IsHuman = this.mode === 'twoplayer' || (this.mode === 'orbs' && this.orbsOpponentType === 'twoplayer');
    if (p2 && p2.roleController) {
      if (p2IsHuman) {
        if (this.keys['arrowleft'] && !this._prevKeyLeft) p2.roleController.activate(this);
        if (this.keys['arrowright'] && !this._prevKeyDown) p2.roleController.activateBonus(this);
      } else if (this.ai) {
        const ball = this.balls[0];
        const rc = p2.roleController;
        if (rc.canActivate() && rc.role.aiShouldUse(p2, this, ball)) {
          if (!this._cpuAbilityPending) {
            this._cpuAbilityPending = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              this._cpuAbilityPending = false;
              if (p2.roleController.canActivate()) p2.roleController.activate(this);
            }, rand(80, 260));
          }
        }
        if (rc.canActivateBonus() && rc.role.bonusAiShouldUse && rc.role.bonusAiShouldUse(p2, this, ball)) {
          if (!this._cpuBonusPending) {
            this._cpuBonusPending = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              this._cpuBonusPending = false;
              if (p2.roleController.canActivateBonus()) p2.roleController.activateBonus(this);
            }, rand(80, 260));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    this._prevKeyLeft = !!this.keys['arrowleft'];
    this._prevKeyDown = !!this.keys['arrowright'];

    if (this.timeDilation && performance.now() > this.timeDilation.until) {
      this.timeDilation = null;
    }
  }

  /* ---------------- Update ---------------- */
  update(dt) {
    dt = Math.min(dt, 1 / 30);
    if (this.state === 'spinning') {
      this.spinner.update(dt);
      this.fx.update(dt); // keep reveal-burst particles animating while the spinner is up
      return;
    }
    if (this.state === 'picking') {
      this.fx.update(dt);
      return; // waiting on the player(s) to choose a role via the DOM picker
    }
    if (this.state === 'countdown') {
      this.countdownTimer -= dt;
      if (this.countdownTimer <= 0) {
        this.countdownVal -= 1;
        if (this.countdownVal <= 0) {
          this.state = 'playing';
          this.sfx.countdown(true);
        } else {
          this.countdownTimer = 1;
          this.sfx.countdown(false);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (this.state === 'creative') {
      this.updateCreative(dt);
      return;
    }
    if (this.state !== 'playing') return;

    const freezeAlive = this.fx.update(dt);
    if (!freezeAlive) return; // hit-stop freeze frame

    this.handleInput(dt);
    for (const p of this.paddles) p.update(dt);
    this.updateRoles(dt);
    this.powerups.update(dt);
    this.obstacles.update(dt);
    this.achievements.update(dt);
    this.updateTempBarriers(dt);
    if (this.mode === 'boss' && this.boss) this.boss.update(dt);

    if (this.mode === 'timeattack') {
      const timeUp = this.timeAttack.update(dt);
      if (timeUp) { this.endTimeAttack(); return; }
    }
    if (this.mode === 'chaos') {
      this.chaosShrinkTimer -= dt;
      if (this.chaosShrinkTimer <= 0) {
        this.chaosShrinkTimer = 9999; // one-shot shrink per match for now
      }
    }

    if (this.mode === 'survival') {
      this.updateSurvival(dt);
      return;
    }

    for (const ball of this.balls) {
      // magnet effect pulls ball toward paddle with active magnet
      for (const p of this.paddles) {
        if (p.magnetTimer > 0) {
          const px = p.side === 'left' ? p.x + p.width : p.x;
          const py = p.centerY();
          const facingRight = p.side === 'left' && ball.vx < 0;
          const facingLeft = p.side === 'right' && ball.vx > 0;
          if (facingRight || facingLeft) {
            const dx = py - ball.y;
            ball.vy += Math.sign(dx) * 220 * dt;
          }
        }
      }
      const ballDt = this.timeDilation ? dt * this.timeDilation.factor : dt;
      ball.update(ballDt);
      this.obstacles.resolveBall(ball);
      this.resolveTempBarriers(ball);

      // wall bounce (top/bottom)
      if (ball.y - ball.radius < 0) {
        ball.y = ball.radius; ball.vy *= -1;
        this.fx.burst(ball.x, 0, '#ffffff', 6, { maxSpeed: 120, maxLife: 0.3 });
        this.sfx.wallHit();
      } else if (ball.y + ball.radius > this.height) {
        ball.y = this.height - ball.radius; ball.vy *= -1;
        this.fx.burst(ball.x, this.height, '#ffffff', 6, { maxSpeed: 120, maxLife: 0.3 });
        this.sfx.wallHit();
      }

      // paddle collision
      for (const p of this.paddles) {
        const approaching = (p.side === 'left' && ball.vx < 0) || (p.side === 'right' && ball.vx > 0);
        if (!approaching) continue;
        if (resolveBallPaddle(ball, p)) {
          this.rallyCount++;
          this.bestRally = Math.max(this.bestRally, this.rallyCount);
          this.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, p.color, 14, { maxSpeed: 200 });
          this.fx.shake(this.settings.screenShake ? 4 : 0, 6);
          this.fx.freeze(0.035);
          this.sfx.paddleHit(1 + Math.min(this.rallyCount * 0.01, 0.5));
          if (this.mode === 'creative') this.stats.creativeRally = true;
          if (this.settings.rolesEnabled && p.roleController) p.roleController.applyOnHit(this, ball);
          if (typeof triggerAbilityHitPhase === 'function') triggerAbilityHitPhase(p);
        } else if (p.twinTimer > 0) {
          // Twin ability: a mirrored ghost paddle (flipped top-to-bottom) also
          // covers this side while the ability is active.
          const twinRect = this.twinRectFor(p);
          if (circleRectCollide(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius, twinRect.x, twinRect.y, twinRect.w, twinRect.h)) {
            const fakePaddle = { side: p.side, x: twinRect.x, y: twinRect.y, width: twinRect.w, vy: -p.vy,
              rect: () => twinRect, sniperCharge: false, game: this };
            if (resolveBallPaddle(ball, fakePaddle)) {
              this.rallyCount++;
              this.bestRally = Math.max(this.bestRally, this.rallyCount);
              this.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, p.color, 14, { maxSpeed: 200 });
              this.sfx.paddleHit(1);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // scoring
      if (ball.x < -40) {
        this.handleGoal('right', ball);
      } else if (ball.x > this.width + 40) {
        this.handleGoal('left', ball);
      }

      if (this.obstacles.allBricksCleared() && this.obstacles.layout === 'bricks') {
        this.stats.bricksCleared = true;
        this.obstacles.setLayout('bricks'); // respawn fresh wall
      }
    }

    this.balls = this.balls.filter(b => b.alive);
    if (this.balls.length === 0 && this.state === 'playing') {
      this.resetRound();
    }
  }
  twinRectFor(p) {
    const h = p.effectiveHeight();
    // mirrored vertically across the court's center
    const mirroredY = this.height - p.y - h;
    return { x: p.x, y: mirroredY, w: p.width, h };
  }
  drawTwinGhost(ctx, p) {
    const r = this.twinRectFor(p);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
    ctx.shadowColor = ROLE_RARITY.epic.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
    ctx.fillStyle = ROLE_RARITY.epic.color;
    roundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }

  endTimeAttack() {
    const g = this;
    g.state = 'gameover';
    g.winner = 'timeattack_end';
    g.stats.gamesPlayed++;
    g.stats.totalPoints += g.paddles[0].score;
    g.saveStats();
    g.history.log({ mode: 'timeattack', score: g.paddles[0].score });
  }

  handleGoal(scoringSide, ball) {
    if (this.mode === 'practice' && scoringSide === 'left') {
      // no opponent to score against in practice — just respawn the ball
      ball.alive = false;
      if (this.balls.filter(b => b.alive).length === 0) this.resetRound(-1);
      return;
    }
    const scorer = this.getPaddleBySide(scoringSide);
    const conceder = this.getPaddleBySide(scoringSide === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left');
    // shield check
    if (conceder && conceder.shieldHits > 0) {
      conceder.shieldHits -= 1;
      ball.alive = false;
      this.sfx.shieldBreak();
      this.fx.text((conceder.side === 'left' ? 80 : this.width - 80), this.height / 2, 'SHIELD BLOCKED!', '#00fff0', 20);
      this.fx.ring(conceder.side === 'left' ? 40 : this.width - 40, ball.y, '#00fff0');
      if (this.balls.filter(b => b.alive).length <= 1) this.resetRound(conceder.side === 'left' ? 1 : -1);
      return;
    }
    ball.alive = false;
    if (this.balls.filter(b => b.alive).length > 0) return; // other balls still in play (multiball)

    scorer.score++;
    this.stats.totalPoints++;
    this.fx.flash(scorer.color, 0.25);
    this.fx.text(this.width / 2, this.height / 2 - 60, scoringSide === 'left' ? 'POINT LEFT' : 'POINT RIGHT', scorer.color, 26);
    this.sfx.score(true);
    this.rallyCount = 0;
    if (this.settings.ghostEnabled && this.ghost.isRecording) this.ghost.stopRecording(this.bestRally);
    if (this.settings.rolesEnabled) {
      if (scorer.roleController) scorer.roleController.applyOnScore();
      if (conceder && conceder.roleController) conceder.roleController.applyOnConcede();
    }

    if (this.mode === 'boss' && this.boss) {
      if (scoringSide === 'left') {
        const dead = this.boss.onPlayerScore();
        if (dead) { this.endMatch('left'); return; }
      } else {
        const dead = this.boss.onBossScore();
        if (dead) { this.endMatch('right'); return; }
      }
      this.resetRound(scoringSide === 'left' ? -1 : 1);
      return;
    }

    if (this.mode === 'timeattack') {
      // no win-score cutoff, just keep playing until clock runs out
      this.resetRound(scoringSide === 'left' ? -1 : 1);
      return;
    }

    if (scorer.score >= this.settings.winScore) {
      this.endMatch(scorer.side);
    } else {
      this.resetRound(scoringSide === 'left' ? -1 : 1);
      if (this.settings.ghostEnabled) this.ghost.startRecording();
    }
  }

  endMatch(winnerSide) {
    this.state = 'gameover';
    this.winner = winnerSide;
    this.stats.gamesPlayed++;
    if (this.mode === 'classic') {
      if (winnerSide === 'left') {
        this.stats.wins++; this.sfx.score(true);
        if (this.settings.aiDifficulty === 'legend') this.stats.beatLegend = true;
      }
      else { this.stats.losses++; }
      this.history.log({ mode: 'classic', difficulty: this.settings.aiDifficulty, won: winnerSide === 'left', scoreA: this.paddles[0].score, scoreB: this.paddles[1].score });
    } else if (this.mode === 'twoplayer') {
      this.history.log({ mode: 'twoplayer', winner: winnerSide === 'left' ? 'Player 1' : 'Player 2', scoreA: this.paddles[0].score, scoreB: this.paddles[1].score });
    } else if (this.mode === 'tournament') {
      if (winnerSide === 'left') {
        this.tournamentRound++;
        if (this.tournamentRound >= this.tournamentOpponents.length) {
          this.stats.tournamentWins++;
          this.winner = 'tournament_champion';
          this.history.log({ mode: 'tournament', won: true });
        }
      } else {
        this.winner = 'tournament_lost';
        this.history.log({ mode: 'tournament', won: false, round: this.tournamentRound + 1 });
      }
    } else if (this.mode === 'chaos') {
      if (winnerSide === 'left') this.stats.chaosWins = (this.stats.chaosWins || 0) + 1;
    } else if (this.mode === 'boss') {
      if (winnerSide === 'left') this.stats.bossDefeated = true;
      this.history.log({ mode: 'boss', won: winnerSide === 'left' });
    }
    this.stats.bestRally = Math.max(this.stats.bestRally || 0, this.bestRally);
    this.saveStats();
    this.fx.shake(this.settings.screenShake ? 10 : 0, 3);
  }

  updateSurvival(dt) {
    this.survivalTime += dt;
    this.survivalBallTimer -= dt;
    if (this.survivalBallTimer <= 0 && this.balls.length < 6) {
      this.spawnBall(-1);
      this.survivalBallTimer = Math.max(3.5, 8 - this.survivalTime / 20);
    }
    const p1 = this.paddles[0];
    for (const ball of this.balls) {
      ball.update(dt);
      this.obstacles.resolveBall(ball);
      if (ball.y - ball.radius < 0) { ball.y = ball.radius; ball.vy *= -1; this.sfx.wallHit(); }
      else if (ball.y + ball.radius > this.height) { ball.y = this.height - ball.radius; ball.vy *= -1; this.sfx.wallHit(); }
      // right wall bounce (no opponent paddle)
      if (ball.x + ball.radius > this.width) { ball.x = this.width - ball.radius; ball.vx *= -1; this.sfx.wallHit(); }
      // left paddle collision
      if (ball.vx < 0 && resolveBallPaddle(ball, p1)) {
        this.rallyCount++;
        this.stats.survivalBest = Math.max(this.stats.survivalBest || 0, this.survivalTime | 0);
        this.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, p1.color, 12, { maxSpeed: 180 });
        this.sfx.paddleHit(1 + Math.min(this.rallyCount * 0.008, 0.6));
      }
      // ball passes left edge -> life lost
      if (ball.x < -40) {
        ball.alive = false;
        p1.lives -= 1;
        this.sfx.score(false);
        this.fx.flash('#ff2266', 0.3);
        this.fx.shake(8, 4);
        if (p1.lives <= 0) {
          this.state = 'gameover';
          this.winner = 'survival_end';
          this.stats.survivalBest = Math.max(this.stats.survivalBest || 0, this.survivalTime | 0);
          this.stats.gamesPlayed++;
          this.saveStats();
          this.history.log({ mode: 'survival', time: Math.floor(this.survivalTime), rally: this.bestRally });
        }
      }
    }
    this.balls = this.balls.filter(b => b.alive);
    if (this.balls.length === 0 && this.state === 'playing') this.spawnBall(-1);
  }

  updateCreative(dt) {
    this.fx.update(dt);
    for (const ball of this.balls) {
      ball.update(dt);
      if (ball.y - ball.radius < 0) { ball.y = ball.radius; ball.vy *= -1; this.sfx.wallHit(); }
      else if (ball.y + ball.radius > this.height) { ball.y = this.height - ball.radius; ball.vy *= -1; this.sfx.wallHit(); }
      if (this.creative.left.collideBall(ball)) { this.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, this.creative.left.color, 10); this.sfx.paddleHit(1); }
      if (this.creative.right.collideBall(ball)) { this.fx.burst(ball.x, ball.y, this.creative.right.color, 10); this.sfx.paddleHit(1.1); }
      if (ball.x < -40 || ball.x > this.width + 40) {
        ball.alive = false;
        this.fx.text(this.width / 2, this.height / 2, ball.x < 0 ? 'RIGHT SCORES' : 'LEFT SCORES', '#fff', 22);
        this.sfx.score(true);
      }
      // keyboard nudge for drawn paddles too
    }
    let dir1 = 0, dir2 = 0;
    if (this.keys['w']) dir1 -= 1;
    if (this.keys['s']) dir1 += 1;
    if (this.keys['arrowup']) dir2 -= 1;
    if (this.keys['arrowdown']) dir2 += 1;
    if (dir1) this.creative.left.moveBy(dir1, dt);
    if (dir2) this.creative.right.moveBy(dir2, dt);

    this.balls = this.balls.filter(b => b.alive);
    if (this.balls.length === 0) this.spawnBall(Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
  }

  /* ---------------- Render ---------------- */
  render() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const w = this.width, h = this.height;
    const th = this.theme();
    ctx.save();
    const shake = this.fx.getShakeOffset();
    ctx.translate(shake.x, shake.y);

    // background gradient
    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h);
    grad.addColorStop(0, th.bg1);
    grad.addColorStop(1, th.bg2);
    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
    ctx.fillRect(-20, -20, w + 40, h + 40);

    this.drawGrid(ctx, th);
    this.drawCenterLine(ctx, th);
    this.obstacles.draw(ctx);
    this.drawTempBarriers(ctx);

    if (this.mode === 'creative' || this.state === 'creative') {
      this.creative.draw(ctx);
    } else {
      if (this.settings.ghostEnabled) this.ghost.draw(ctx, this);
      for (const p of this.paddles) {
        if (p.isWall) {
          ctx.save();
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
          ctx.fillRect(p.x, p.y, p.width, p.effectiveHeight());
          ctx.restore();
        } else {
          if (p.twinTimer > 0) this.drawTwinGhost(ctx, p);
          drawPaddleWithSkin(ctx, this, p);
        }
      }
    }

    this.powerups.draw(ctx);
    for (const ball of this.balls) ball.draw(ctx);
    this.fx.draw(ctx);

    this.drawScores(ctx, th);
    if (this.mode === 'boss' && this.boss) this.boss.drawHud(ctx, w);
    if (this.mode === 'timeattack') this.timeAttack.drawHud(ctx, w);
    this.achievements.drawToast(ctx, w);
    if (this.settings.rolesEnabled) this.drawRoleHud(ctx, w, h);

    if (this.state === 'countdown') this.drawCountdown(ctx, th);
    if (this.state === 'spinning') {
      this.spinner.draw(ctx, w, h);
      this.fx.draw(ctx); // redraw particles on top of the spinner overlay so reveal bursts stay visible
    }

    ctx.restore();
    this.fx.drawFlash(ctx, w, h);

    if (this.settings.showFps) this.drawFps(ctx);
  }

  drawRoleHud(ctx, w, h) {
    for (const p of this.paddles) {
      if (!p.roleController || !p.roleController.hasActiveAbility()) continue;
      const rc = p.roleController;
      const rarity = ROLE_RARITY[rc.role.rarity];
      const x = p.side === 'left' ? 46 : w - 46;
      const y = h - 46;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(x, y);
      // cooldown ring
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 4;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 22, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      const ratio = rc.cooldownRatio();
      ctx.strokeStyle = ratio >= 1 ? rarity.color : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
      ctx.shadowColor = rarity.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = ratio >= 1 ? 14 : 0;
      ctx.lineWidth = 4;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(0, 0, 22, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + TAU * ratio);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      ctx.fillStyle = ratio >= 1 ? '#fff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
      ctx.font = '18px sans-serif';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.fillText(rc.role.icon, 0, 1);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  drawGrid(ctx, th) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba(th.accent, 0.06);
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    const step = 40;
    for (let x = 0; x < this.width; x += step) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, this.height); ctx.stroke();
    }
    for (let y = 0; y < this.height; y += step) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(this.width, y); ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  drawCenterLine(ctx, th) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba(th.accent, 0.35);
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.setLineDash([14, 14]);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(this.width / 2, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(this.width / 2, this.height);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  drawScores(ctx, th) {
    if (this.paddles.length === 0) return;
    if (this.mode === 'survival') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillStyle = th.p1;
      ctx.shadowColor = th.p1; ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
      ctx.font = '800 42px Orbitron, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(Math.floor(this.survivalTime) + 's', this.width / 2, 60);
      ctx.font = '600 16px Rajdhani, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText('♥'.repeat(Math.max(0, this.paddles[0].lives)), this.width / 2, 88);
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === 'creative' || this.mode === 'practice') return;
    const scoreY = this.mode === 'boss' ? 130 : 68;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '800 56px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
    ctx.shadowColor = this.paddles[0].color;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.paddles[0].color;
    ctx.fillText(String(this.paddles[0].score), this.width / 2 - 90, scoreY);
    if (this.paddles[1]) {
      ctx.shadowColor = this.paddles[1].color;
      ctx.fillStyle = this.paddles[1].color;
      ctx.fillText(String(this.paddles[1].score), this.width / 2 + 90, scoreY);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  drawCountdown(ctx, th) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.font = '900 120px Orbitron, sans-serif';
    ctx.shadowColor = th.accent;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 30;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    const label = this.countdownVal > 0 ? String(this.countdownVal) : 'GO!';
    const scale = 1 + (1 - this.countdownTimer) * 0.3;
    ctx.translate(this.width / 2, this.height / 2);
    ctx.scale(scale, scale);
    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(this.countdownTimer, 0, 1);
    ctx.fillText(label, 0, 0);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  drawFps(ctx) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0';
    ctx.font = '12px monospace';
    ctx.fillText('FPS: ' + (this.fps || 0), 8, this.height - 8);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ---------------- Loop ---------------- */
  tick(now) {
    const dt = Math.min((now - this.lastTime) / 1000, 0.05);
    this.lastTime = now;
    if (this.settings.showFps) {
      this.fpsHistory.push(1 / dt);
      if (this.fpsHistory.length > 30) this.fpsHistory.shift();
      this.fps = Math.round(this.fpsHistory.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / this.fpsHistory.length);
    }
    this.update(dt);
    this.render();
    requestAnimationFrame((t) => this.tick(t));
  }
  start() {
    requestAnimationFrame((t) => { this.lastTime = t; this.tick(t); });
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: UI LAYER
   Manages all DOM overlay screens: main menu, mode select,
   settings, pause, game over, creative toolbar, tournament HUD
   ============================================================ */

class UIManager {
  constructor(game) {
    this.game = game;
    this.root = document.getElementById('ui-root');
    this.screens = {};
    this.currentScreen = null;
    this.buildScreens();
    this.bindGlobalKeys();
  }

  q(sel, root = document) { return root.querySelector(sel); }
  qa(sel, root = document) { return Array.from(root.querySelectorAll(sel)); }

  show(name) {
    for (const key in this.screens) {
      this.screens[key].classList.toggle('active', key === name);
    }
    this.currentScreen = name;
  }
  hideAll() {
    for (const key in this.screens) this.screens[key].classList.remove('active');
    this.currentScreen = null;
  }

  buildScreens() {
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="screen active" id="screen-main">

          <button class="menu-btn primary" data-action="rematch">Play Again</button>
          <button class="menu-btn ghost" data-action="quit-main">Quit to Menu</button>

      <div class="creative-toolbar" id="creative-toolbar">
        <button class="icon-btn" data-action="clear-left" title="Clear left drawing">🧹 P1</button>
        <div class="creative-hint">Draw on your half to make a paddle • W/S and ↑/↓ to slide</div>

    this.screens = {
      main: this.q('#screen-main'),
      modes: this.q('#screen-modes'),
      difficulty: this.q('#screen-difficulty'),
      orbsOpponent: this.q('#screen-orbs-opponent'),
      settings: this.q('#screen-settings'),
      stats: this.q('#screen-stats'),
      howto: this.q('#screen-howto'),
      pause: this.q('#screen-pause'),
      gameover: this.q('#screen-gameover'),
    };

    this.creativeToolbar = this.q('#creative-toolbar');

    this.buildThemeGrid();
    this.buildPowerupToggles();
    this.buildArenaToggles();
    this.buildSkinSelects();
    this.bindSettingsInputs();
    this.bindClicks();
    this.refreshSettingsUI();

    this.hud.style.display = 'none';
    this.creativeToolbar.style.display = 'none';
  }

  buildThemeGrid() {
    const grid = this.q('#theme-grid');
    grid.innerHTML = '';
    for (const key in THEMES) {
      const th = THEMES[key];
      const el = document.createElement('button');
      el.className = 'theme-swatch';
      el.dataset.theme = key;
      el.style.background = `linear-gradient(135deg, ${th.p1}, ${th.p2})`;
      el.title = th.name;
      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.game.settings.theme = key;
        this.game.saveSettings();
        this.game.sfx.menuBlip();
        this.refreshSettingsUI();
      });
      grid.appendChild(el);
    }
  }
  buildPowerupToggles() {
    const list = this.q('#powerup-toggle-list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    for (const type of POWERUP_TYPES) {
      const label = document.createElement('label');
      label.className = 'field checkbox powerup-toggle';
      label.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" data-powerup="${type.id}"><span style="color:${type.color}">${type.icon} ${type.label}</span>`;
      list.appendChild(label);
      label.querySelector('input').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        this.game.settings.enabledPowerups[type.id] = e.target.checked;
        this.game.saveSettings();
      });
    }
  }
  buildArenaToggles() {
    const list = this.q('#arena-toggle-list');
    if (!list) return;
    list.innerHTML = '';
    for (const layout of ARENA_LAYOUTS) {
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'diff-card arena-card';
      btn.dataset.arena = layout.id;
      btn.innerHTML = `<span class="diff-name">${layout.label}</span><span class="diff-sub">${layout.desc}</span>`;
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.game.settings.arenaLayout = layout.id;
        this.game.saveSettings();
        this.game.sfx.menuBlip();
        this.refreshSettingsUI();
      });
      list.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }
  buildSkinSelects() {
    const p1 = this.q('#set-skinP1');
    const p2 = this.q('#set-skinP2');
    if (!p1 || !p2) return;
    const options = Object.keys(PADDLE_SKINS).map(key => `<option value="${key}">${PADDLE_SKINS[key].label}</option>`).join('');
    p1.innerHTML = options;
    p2.innerHTML = options;
    p1.addEventListener('change', () => { this.game.settings.skinP1 = p1.value; this.game.saveSettings(); });
    p2.addEventListener('change', () => { this.game.settings.skinP2 = p2.value; this.game.saveSettings(); });
  }

  bindSettingsInputs() {
    const g = this.game;
    const bindRange = (id, key, fmt = (v) => v) => {
      const el = this.q('#' + id);
      el.addEventListener('input', () => {
        g.settings[key] = Number(el.value);
        this.q('#val-' + key).textContent = fmt(g.settings[key]);
        g.saveSettings();
        this.applyLiveSettings();
      });
    };
    bindRange('set-paddleSpeed', 'paddleSpeed');
    bindRange('set-ballSpeed', 'ballSpeed');
    bindRange('set-paddleSize', 'paddleSize', (v) => v + '%');
    bindRange('set-winScore', 'winScore');
    bindRange('set-timeAttackDuration', 'timeAttackDuration', (v) => v + 's');
    bindRange('set-sfxVolume', 'sfxVolume', (v) => v + '%');
    bindRange('set-glowIntensity', 'glowIntensity', (v) => v + '%');

    const bindCheck = (id, key, onChange) => {
      const el = this.q('#' + id);
      el.addEventListener('change', () => {
        g.settings[key] = el.checked;
        g.saveSettings();
        if (onChange) onChange(el.checked);
      });
    };
    bindCheck('set-powerupsEnabled', 'powerupsEnabled');
    bindCheck('set-screenShake', 'screenShake');
    bindCheck('set-soundOn', 'soundOn', (v) => g.sfx.setVolume(v ? g.settings.sfxVolume / 100 : 0));
    bindCheck('set-musicOn', 'musicOn', (v) => g.sfx.toggleMusic(v));
    bindCheck('set-showFps', 'showFps');
    bindCheck('set-ghostEnabled', 'ghostEnabled');
    bindCheck('set-rolesEnabled', 'rolesEnabled', () => this.syncAnyRolesAvailability());
    bindCheck('set-anyRolesEnabled', 'anyRolesEnabled');
    bindCheck('set-colorblindMode', 'colorblindMode');
    bindCheck('set-reducedMotion', 'reducedMotion');
  }
  syncAnyRolesAvailability() {
    const enabled = this.game.settings.rolesEnabled;
    const el = this.q('#set-anyRolesEnabled');
    el.disabled = !enabled;
    if (!enabled && el.checked) {
      el.checked = false;
      this.game.settings.anyRolesEnabled = false;
      this.game.saveSettings();
    }
    el.closest('.field').style.opacity = enabled ? '1' : '0.4';
  }
  applyLiveSettings() {
    const g = this.game;
    g.sfx.setVolume(g.settings.soundOn ? g.settings.sfxVolume / 100 : 0);
    for (const p of g.paddles) if (p.speed) p.speed = g.settings.paddleSpeed;
  }
  refreshSettingsUI() {
    const s = this.game.settings;
    this.q('#set-paddleSpeed').value = s.paddleSpeed; this.q('#val-paddleSpeed').textContent = s.paddleSpeed;
    this.q('#set-ballSpeed').value = s.ballSpeed; this.q('#val-ballSpeed').textContent = s.ballSpeed;
    this.q('#set-paddleSize').value = s.paddleSize; this.q('#val-paddleSize').textContent = s.paddleSize + '%';
    this.q('#set-winScore').value = s.winScore; this.q('#val-winScore').textContent = s.winScore;
    this.q('#set-timeAttackDuration').value = s.timeAttackDuration; this.q('#val-timeAttackDuration').textContent = s.timeAttackDuration + 's';
    this.q('#set-sfxVolume').value = Math.round(s.sfxVolume * 100); this.q('#val-sfxVolume').textContent = Math.round(s.sfxVolume * 100) + '%';
    this.q('#set-glowIntensity').value = Math.round(s.glowIntensity * 100); this.q('#val-glowIntensity').textContent = Math.round(s.glowIntensity * 100) + '%';
    this.q('#set-powerupsEnabled').checked = s.powerupsEnabled;
    this.q('#set-screenShake').checked = s.screenShake;
    this.q('#set-soundOn').checked = s.soundOn;
    this.q('#set-musicOn').checked = s.musicOn;
    this.q('#set-showFps').checked = s.showFps;
    this.q('#set-ghostEnabled').checked = s.ghostEnabled;
    this.q('#set-rolesEnabled').checked = s.rolesEnabled;
    this.q('#set-anyRolesEnabled').checked = s.anyRolesEnabled;
    this.syncAnyRolesAvailability();
    this.q('#set-colorblindMode').checked = s.colorblindMode;
    this.q('#set-reducedMotion').checked = s.reducedMotion;
    this.q('#set-skinP1').value = s.skinP1;
    this.q('#set-skinP2').value = s.skinP2;
    this.qa('.theme-swatch').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('selected', el.dataset.theme === s.theme));
    this.qa('[data-powerup]').forEach(el => { el.checked = s.enabledPowerups[el.dataset.powerup] !== false; });
    this.qa('[data-arena]').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('selected', el.dataset.arena === s.arenaLayout));
  }

  refreshStats() {
    const s = this.game.stats;
    const grid = this.q('#stats-grid');
    const winRate = s.gamesPlayed ? Math.round((s.wins / Math.max(1, s.wins + s.losses)) * 100) : 0;
    grid.innerHTML = `
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.gamesPlayed || 0}</div><div class="stat-label">Games Played</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.wins || 0}</div><div class="stat-label">Wins</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.losses || 0}</div><div class="stat-label">Losses</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${winRate}%</div><div class="stat-label">Win Rate</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.bestRally || 0}</div><div class="stat-label">Best Rally</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.tournamentWins || 0}</div><div class="stat-label">Tournaments Won</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.survivalBest || 0}s</div><div class="stat-label">Survival Best</div></div>
      <div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-val">${s.totalPoints || 0}</div><div class="stat-label">Total Points</div></div>
    `;

    const prog = this.game.achievements.progress();
    this.q('#achievement-progress').textContent = `(${prog.unlocked}/${prog.total})`;
    const achGrid = this.q('#achievements-grid');
    achGrid.innerHTML = ACHIEVEMENTS.map(a => {
      const unlocked = !!this.game.achievements.unlocked[a.id];
      return `<div class="achievement-card ${unlocked ? 'unlocked' : 'locked'}" title="${a.desc}">
        <div class="achievement-icon">${unlocked ? a.icon : '🔒'}</div>
        <div class="achievement-name">${a.name}</div>
        <div class="achievement-desc">${a.desc}</div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const histList = this.q('#history-list');
    const recent = this.game.history.recent(10);
    if (recent.length === 0) {
      histList.innerHTML = `<div class="history-empty">No matches played yet.</div>`;
    } else {
      histList.innerHTML = recent.map(e => {
        const f = formatHistoryEntry(e);
        return `<div class="history-row"><span class="history-date">${f.dateStr} ${f.timeStr}</span><span class="history-result">${f.result}</span></div>`;
      }).join('');
    }
  }

  bindClicks() {
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const actionEl = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
      if (actionEl) this.handleAction(actionEl.dataset.action);
      const modeEl = e.target.closest('[data-mode]');
      if (modeEl) this.handleModeSelect(modeEl.dataset.mode);
      const diffEl = e.target.closest('[data-diff]');
      if (diffEl) this.handleDifficultySelect(diffEl.dataset.diff);
      const orbsOppEl = e.target.closest('[data-orbs-opponent]');
      if (orbsOppEl) this.handleOrbsOpponentSelect(orbsOppEl.dataset.orbsOpponent);
    });
  }

  handleAction(action) {
    const g = this.game;
    g.sfx.resume();
    switch (action) {
      case 'goto-main': g.sfx.menuBlip(); this.showMain(); break;
      case 'goto-modes': g.sfx.menuBlip(); this.show('modes'); break;
      case 'goto-settings': g.sfx.menuBlip(); this.refreshSettingsUI(); this.show('settings'); break;
      case 'goto-stats': g.sfx.menuBlip(); this.refreshStats(); this.show('stats'); break;
      case 'goto-howto': g.sfx.menuBlip(); this.show('howto'); break;
      case 'reset-settings':
        g.sfx.menuBlip();
        Object.assign(g.settings, DEFAULT_SETTINGS());
        g.saveSettings(); this.refreshSettingsUI();
        break;
      case 'resume': g.sfx.menuConfirm(); this.resumeGame(); break;
      case 'restart-match': g.sfx.menuConfirm(); this.restartMatch(); break;
      case 'rematch': g.sfx.menuConfirm(); this.restartMatch(); break;
      case 'quit-main': g.sfx.menuBlip(); this.quitToMain(); break;
      case 'clear-left': g.creative.left.clear(); g.sfx.menuBlip(); break;
      case 'clear-right': g.creative.right.clear(); g.sfx.menuBlip(); break;
    }
  }

  handleModeSelect(mode) {
    const g = this.game;
    g.sfx.menuConfirm();
    this.pendingMode = mode;
    if (mode === 'classic' || mode === 'chaos' || mode === 'timeattack') {
      this.show('difficulty');
      return;
    }
    if (mode === 'orbs') {
      this.show('orbsOpponent');
      return;
    }
    this.launchMode(mode);
  }
  handleDifficultySelect(diff) {
    this.game.settings.aiDifficulty = diff;
    this.game.saveSettings();
    if (this.pendingMode === 'orbs') {
      this.launchOrbs(this.pendingOrbsOpponent || 'cpu');
      return;
    }
    this.launchMode(this.pendingMode);
  }
  handleOrbsOpponentSelect(opponentType) {
    this.game.sfx.menuConfirm();
    this.pendingOrbsOpponent = opponentType;
    if (opponentType === 'cpu') {
      this.show('difficulty');
      return;
    }
    this.launchOrbs('twoplayer');
  }
  launchOrbs(opponentType) {
    const g = this.game;
    this.hideAll();
    g.setupOrbs(opponentType);
    this.setModeLabel('Orbs Mode — ' + (opponentType === 'cpu' ? 'vs ' + g.settings.aiDifficulty.toUpperCase() : '2 Player'));
    this.hud.style.display = 'flex';
    this.creativeToolbar.style.display = 'none';
  }

  launchMode(mode) {
    const g = this.game;
    this.hideAll();
    if (mode === 'classic') { g.setupClassic(); this.setModeLabel('Classic — vs ' + g.settings.aiDifficulty.toUpperCase()); }
    else if (mode === 'twoplayer') { g.setupTwoPlayer(); this.setModeLabel('Local 2-Player'); }
    else if (mode === 'tournament') { g.setupTournament(); this.setModeLabel('Tournament — Round 1/4'); }
    else if (mode === 'survival') { g.setupSurvival(); this.setModeLabel('Survival'); }
    else if (mode === 'creative') { g.setupCreative(); this.setModeLabel('Creative Draw Mode'); }
    else if (mode === 'chaos') { g.setupChaos(); this.setModeLabel('Chaos — ' + g.obstacles.layout.toUpperCase()); }
    else if (mode === 'timeattack') { g.setupTimeAttack(); this.setModeLabel('Time Attack — ' + g.settings.timeAttackDuration + 's'); }
    else if (mode === 'boss') { g.setupBoss(); this.setModeLabel('Boss Rush'); }
    else if (mode === 'practice') { g.setupPractice(); this.setModeLabel('Practice Wall'); }

    this.hud.style.display = 'flex';
    this.creativeToolbar.style.display = mode === 'creative' ? 'flex' : 'none';
  }
  setModeLabel(text) { this.q('#hud-mode-label').textContent = text; }

  showMain() {
    this.hideAll();
    this.show('main');
    this.hud.style.display = 'none';
    this.creativeToolbar.style.display = 'none';
    this.game.state = 'menu';
  }

  pauseGame() {
    const g = this.game;
    if (g.state !== 'playing' && g.state !== 'countdown') return;
    g.prevState = g.state;
    g.state = 'paused';
    this.show('pause');
  }
  resumeGame() {
    const g = this.game;
    g.state = g.prevState || 'playing';
    this.hideAll();
  }
  restartMatch() {
    const g = this.game;
    this.hideAll();
    if (g.mode === 'classic') g.setupClassic();
    else if (g.mode === 'twoplayer') g.setupTwoPlayer();
    else if (g.mode === 'tournament') g.setupTournament();
    else if (g.mode === 'survival') g.setupSurvival();
    else if (g.mode === 'creative') g.setupCreative();
    else if (g.mode === 'chaos') g.setupChaos();
    else if (g.mode === 'timeattack') g.setupTimeAttack();
    else if (g.mode === 'boss') g.setupBoss();
    else if (g.mode === 'practice') g.setupPractice();
    else if (g.mode === 'orbs') g.setupOrbs();
  }
  quitToMain() {
    const g = this.game;
    g.sfx.toggleMusic(false);
    g.settings.musicOn = false;
    this.showMain();
  }

  /* ---------------- Role Picker (Any Roles) ---------------- */
  showRolePicker(side, onPick) {
    const g = this.game;
    const screen = this.q('#screen-role-picker');
    const search = this.q('#role-picker-search');
    const title = this.q('#role-picker-title');

    // Support both sides picking at once (human-vs-human): each call registers
    // its callback, but only the FIRST call (when no picker is currently shown)
    // actually paints the grid — a second call while one is already open just
    // queues its callback silently, and the click handler advances to it once
    // the first side resolves. This prevents the second call from clobbering
    // the first side's still-unresolved UI.
    this._rolePickerCallbacks = this._rolePickerCallbacks || {};
    this._rolePickerCallbacks[side] = onPick;

    const alreadyOpen = screen.classList.contains('active');
    if (alreadyOpen) return; // queued; will be shown when the active side resolves

    this._rolePickerActiveSide = side;
    title.textContent = side === 'left' ? 'Player 1 — Choose Your Role' : 'Player 2 — Choose Your Role';
    search.value = '';
    this.renderRolePickerGrid('');
    search.oninput = () => this.renderRolePickerGrid(search.value);
    screen.classList.add('active');
  }
  renderRolePickerGrid(filterText) {
    const grid = this.q('#role-picker-grid');
    const q = filterText.trim().toLowerCase();
    const filtered = PADDLE_ROLES.filter(r =>
      !q || r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.rarity.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (r.desc || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
    const order = { mythic: 0, legendary: 1, epic: 2, rare: 3, common: 4 };
    filtered.sort((a, b) => (order[a.rarity] - order[b.rarity]) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));

    if (filtered.length === 0) {
      grid.innerHTML = `<div class="role-picker-empty">No roles match "${filterText}"</div>`;
      return;
    }
    grid.innerHTML = filtered.map(role => {
      const rarity = ROLE_RARITY[role.rarity];
      return `<button class="role-pick-card" data-role-id="${role.id}" style="--rc:${rarity.color}">
        <span class="role-pick-icon">${role.icon}</span>
        <span class="role-pick-name">${role.name}</span>
        <span class="role-pick-rarity">${rarity.label}</span>
      </button>`;
    }).join('');
    grid.querySelectorAll('[data-role-id]').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const role = getRole(btn.dataset.roleId);
        const side = this._rolePickerActiveSide;
        this.game.sfx.menuConfirm();
        const cb = this._rolePickerCallbacks && this._rolePickerCallbacks[side];
        if (cb) cb(role);
        // switch the visible grid to whichever side is still pending, if any;
        // the game itself (maybeFinishPicking) decides when to fully close
        const otherSide = side === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left';
        const otherPending = this.game.pendingPicks && this.game.pendingPicks[otherSide] === false;
        if (otherPending) {
          this._rolePickerActiveSide = otherSide;
          this.q('#role-picker-title').textContent = otherSide === 'left' ? 'Player 1 — Choose Your Role' : 'Player 2 — Choose Your Role';
          this.q('#role-picker-search').value = '';
          this.renderRolePickerGrid('');
        }
      }, { once: true });
    });
  }
  hideRolePicker() {
    this.q('#screen-role-picker').classList.remove('active');
    this._rolePickerCallbacks = {};
  }

  showGameOver() {
    const g = this.game;
    const title = this.q('#gameover-title');
    const sub = this.q('#gameover-sub');
    if (g.mode === 'survival') {
      title.textContent = 'Run Ended';
      sub.textContent = `You survived ${Math.floor(g.survivalTime)} seconds with a best rally of ${g.bestRally}.`;
    } else if (g.mode === 'tournament') {
      if (g.winner === 'tournament_champion') {
        title.textContent = '🏆 CHAMPION 🏆';
        sub.textContent = 'You defeated all four opponents. Legendary.';
      } else if (g.winner === 'tournament_lost') {
        title.textContent = 'Eliminated';
        sub.textContent = `You fell in round ${g.tournamentRound + 1} of 4.`;
      } else {
        title.textContent = `Round ${g.tournamentRound} Cleared`;
        sub.textContent = 'Advancing to the next opponent...';
        setTimeout(() => { this.hideAll(); g.startTournamentRound(); }, 1800);
        return;
      }
    } else {
      const winnerLabel = g.mode === 'twoplayer'
        ? (g.winner === 'left' ? 'Player 1 Wins!' : 'Player 2 Wins!')
        : (g.winner === 'left' ? 'You Win!' : 'AI Wins');
      title.textContent = winnerLabel;
      sub.textContent = `Final score ${g.paddles[0].score} - ${g.paddles[1].score}`;
    }
    this.show('gameover');
  }

  bindGlobalKeys() {
    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
      this.game.keys[k] = true;
      if (k === 'escape') {
        if (this.game.state === 'playing' || this.game.state === 'countdown') this.pauseGame();
        else if (this.game.state === 'paused') this.resumeGame();
      }
      if (['arrowup', 'arrowdown', ' '].includes(k)) e.preventDefault();
    });
    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
      this.game.keys[e.key.toLowerCase()] = false;
    });
    this.q('#btn-pause').addEventListener('click', () => this.pauseGame());
  }

  watchGameOver() {
    // called each frame by main loop to detect transition
    if (this.game.state === 'gameover' && this.currentScreen !== 'gameover') {
      this.showGameOver();
    }
  }
}
/* ============================================================
   NEON PONG :: INPUT + BOOTSTRAP
   ============================================================ */

function setupPointerInput(game, canvas) {
  function toCanvasCoords(clientX, clientY) {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = (clientX - rect.left) / rect.width * game.width;
    const y = (clientY - rect.top) / rect.height * game.height;
    return { x, y };
  }

  let activePointers = new Map();

  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
    game.sfx.resume();
    const { x, y } = toCanvasCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    activePointers.set(e.pointerId, { x, y });
    canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);

    if (game.state === 'creative') {
      game.creative.pointerDown(x, y);
    } else if (game.state === 'playing') {
      if (game.mode === 'twoplayer') {
        if (x < game.width / 2) game.touchP1Target = y; else game.touchP2Target = y;
      } else {
        game.touchP1Target = y;
      }
    }
  });

  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    if (!activePointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;
    const { x, y } = toCanvasCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    activePointers.set(e.pointerId, { x, y });

    if (game.state === 'creative') {
      game.creative.pointerMove(x, y);
    } else if (game.state === 'playing') {
      if (game.mode === 'twoplayer') {
        if (x < game.width / 2) game.touchP1Target = y; else game.touchP2Target = y;
      } else {
        game.touchP1Target = y;
      }
    }
  });

  function release(e) {
    activePointers.delete(e.pointerId);
    if (game.state === 'creative') {
      game.creative.pointerUp();
    }
    if (activePointers.size === 0) {
      game.touchP1Target = null;
      game.touchP2Target = null;
    }
  }
  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
  canvas.addEventListener('pointerleave', (e) => {
    if (game.state !== 'creative') return; // keep touch-drag paddle control sticky
  });

  // prevent default touch scrolling on canvas
  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
}

function fitCanvas(canvas, game) {
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  function resize() {
    const maxW = wrap.clientWidth * 0.98;
    const maxH = wrap.clientHeight * 0.98;
    const targetRatio = game.width / game.height;
    let w = maxW, h = maxW / targetRatio;
    if (h > maxH) { h = maxH; w = maxH * targetRatio; }
    canvas.style.width = w + 'px';
    canvas.style.height = h + 'px';

    // On very narrow/portrait viewports the 16:9-ish board letterboxes hard.
    // Show a gentle rotate hint instead of wasting most of the screen —
    // but only while a match is actually being played, not on menu screens.
    const rotateHint = document.getElementById('rotate-hint');
    if (rotateHint) {
      const veryNarrow = wrap.clientWidth < wrap.clientHeight * 0.85 && wrap.clientWidth < 560;
      const inGameplay = game.state === 'playing' || game.state === 'countdown' || game.state === 'creative';
      rotateHint.style.display = (veryNarrow && inGameplay) ? 'flex' : 'none';
    }
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => setTimeout(resize, 200));
  // re-check each frame too, since gameplay state changes without a resize event
  setInterval(resize, 400);
  resize();
}

/* ---------------- Fullscreen ---------------- */
function toggleFullscreen() {
  const el = document.getElementById('app');
  const isFullscreen = document.fullscreenElement || document.webkitFullscreenElement;
  if (!isFullscreen) {
    const req = el.requestFullscreen || el.webkitRequestFullscreen || el.msRequestFullscreen;
    if (req) req.call(el).catch(() => {});
  } else {
    const exit = document.exitFullscreen || document.webkitExitFullscreen || document.msExitFullscreen;
    if (exit) exit.call(document).catch(() => {});
  }
}
function updateFullscreenButtons() {
  const isFullscreen = !!(document.fullscreenElement || document.webkitFullscreenElement);
  document.querySelectorAll('.corner-fullscreen, #btn-fullscreen-hud').forEach(btn => {
    btn.textContent = isFullscreen ? '⛶' : '⛶';
    btn.title = isFullscreen ? 'Exit Fullscreen' : 'Fullscreen';
    btn.classList.toggle('is-fullscreen', isFullscreen);
  });
}

/* ---------------- Boot ---------------- */
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
  canvas.width = 960;
  canvas.height = 540;
  const game = new Game(canvas);
  const ui = new UIManager(game);
  game.ui = ui;

  setupPointerInput(game, canvas);
  fitCanvas(canvas, game);

  document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (e.target.closest('#btn-fullscreen-main') || e.target.closest('#btn-fullscreen-hud')) {
      game.sfx.menuBlip();
      toggleFullscreen();
    }
  });
  ['fullscreenchange', 'webkitfullscreenchange'].forEach(ev => {
    document.addEventListener(ev, () => { updateFullscreenButtons(); setTimeout(() => fitCanvas(canvas, game), 100); });
  });
  updateFullscreenButtons();

  // watch for game-over transitions each frame via a light wrapper
  const originalRender = game.render.bind(game);
  game.render = function () {
    originalRender();
    ui.watchGameOver();
  };

  game.start();
  window.__game = game; // expose for debugging / potential future integrations

  // resume audio context on first interaction (autoplay policies)
  const resumeOnce = () => { game.sfx.resume(); window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', resumeOnce); };
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown', resumeOnce);
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
